<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视觉模型是认知机器人在工业和家庭应用中的关键驱动力，但现有方法在训练数据效率、AI架构精度、可扩展性以及跨领域泛化方面仍面临挑战，亟需更强大有效的方法。

Method: 通过综述当前最先进方法中的限制与趋势，识别领域差距问题，并提出通过链接模拟与现实世界的训练数据生成来弥合这一差距。

Result: 指出现有方法在精度限制、可扩展性和跨领域泛化方面的不足，并介绍了在弥合模拟-现实领域差距方面的初步工作进展。

Conclusion: AI视觉模型在认知机器人中的应用仍需克服领域差距等关键挑战，通过改进训练数据生成策略（如模拟与现实数据结合）是可行的研究方向。

Abstract: AI vision models are a driving factor for the potential use case scenarios of cognitive robotics within in the industry and household applications. A large array of methods from semantic environment analysis towards 6D and grasping pose estimation have been proposed based on the latest AI achievements. However, such advancements require further strong and efficient methods w.r.t. training data and AI-architectures, which are capable in synergy to tackle current challenges, precision limits, and scalability beyond domain gaps. In this paper, we discuss these current limits and trends in the related state-of-the-art which are challenging those. Further we discuss our current work in progress on bridging the domain gap between simulations and real world applications by linking those in the training data generation.

</details>


### [71] [Co-VLA: Coordination-Aware Structured Action Modeling for Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20285)
*Yandong Wang,Jiaqian Yu,Xiongfeng Peng,Lu Xu,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Jaewook Yoo,Dongwook Lee,Daehyun Ji,Mingbo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Co-VLA 通过在VLA模型中引入显式结构先验（结构化动作专家SAE和潜在感知控制器LAC），显著提升了双臂机器人在紧耦合任务中的协调能力，成功率提升27%，OOD场景性能翻倍（13%→27%），任务完成时间减少25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在双臂操作中依赖隐式协调，当任务耦合紧密且执行约束严格时，隐式协调不足以确保可靠、可解释且稳定的行为。

Method: 提出Co-VLA框架：1) 将VLA的单一动作头替换为结构化动作专家（SAE），包含共享潜变量（编码任务级协调意图）和残差潜变量（捕获各臂执行调整）；2) 引入模块化协调感知损失；3) 部署时使用潜在感知控制器（LAC）实时调节同步强度、执行不对称性、平滑性和安全约束，工作在关节指令层面。

Result: 紧协调任务成功率提升27%；OOD真实场景从13%提升至27%（性能翻倍）；任务完成时间最多减少25%。

Conclusion: 显式引入协调结构先验能有效提升VLA模型在双臂紧耦合任务中的性能，且LAC可与标准控制管线兼容。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models show strong capabilities in single and dual-arm robotic manipulation. Prior works show coordinated bimanual behaviors can emerge from end-to-end learning, leveraging large vision-language backbones with continuous action prediction. However, as bimanual tasks become tightly coupled and execution constraints become critical, implicit coordination alone is insufficient to ensure reliable, interpretable, and stable behavior.
  In this work, we propose Co-VLA, a coordination-aware bimanual manipulation framework introducing explicit structural priors into VLA models. We instantiate our method on a state-of-the-art vision-language backbone by replacing its monolithic action head with a Structured Action Expert (SAE) designed for bimanual coordination. Specifically, we introduce explicit structure at the action generation level with a modular coordination-aware loss that shapes shared and residual latents according to task-specific structures. The shared latent encodes task-level coordination intent, while residual latents capture execution adjustments for each arm.
  At deployment, a Latent-Aware Controller (LAC) interprets the learned representations to modulate synchronization strength, execution asymmetry, smoothness, and safety constraints in real time. LAC operates at the joint-command level and remains compatible with standard control pipelines without requiring force or impedance control. Experiments across simulation and real-world benchmarks show Co-VLA significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, achieving a 27% success rate gain in tight-coordination tasks, more than doubling performance in OOD real-world scenarios (from 13% to 27%), and reducing task completion time by up to 25%.

</details>


### [72] [Towards 3D karst underwater scene reconstruction from rotating sonar data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20322)
*Georgios Evangelos Margaritis,Lionel Lapierre,Simon Rohou,Zhi Yan,Andreas Nüchter,François Goulette*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合连续时间SLAM轨迹校正和两阶段深度学习表面重建的管道，用于从声纳剖面仪数据重建水下喀斯特管道，生成可沉浸式导航的3D网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喀斯特含水层提供重要的淡水资源，但由于其复杂且难以理解的地下几何结构，存在显著的地质灾害风险。水下勘测中的声纳数据稀疏且有噪声，导航估计存在漂移，标准3D重建方法效果受限。

Method: 采用连续时间SLAM方法校正轨迹漂移，结合新颖的两阶段深度学习方法进行表面重建，最终生成用于水文地质分析的沉浸式可导航3D网格。

Result: 成功从稀疏且有噪声的声纳剖面仪数据中重建出水下喀斯特管道的3D网格，有效校正了轨迹漂移问题。

Conclusion: 该管道能够有效处理水下喀斯特环境的复杂几何结构，为水文地质分析提供高质量的3D重建结果，解决了传统方法在稀疏噪声数据和轨迹漂移下的局限性。

Abstract: Karst aquifers provide critical freshwater resources but pose significant hazards due to their complex and poorly understood subsurface geometry. Mapping these environments is challenging because sonar data from underwater exploration is sparse and noisy, while navigation estimates suffer from drift limiting standard 3D reconstruction methods. We present a pipeline for reconstructing underwater karst conduits from a sonar profiler. We combine a continuous-time SLAM approach to correct trajectory drift with a novel two-stage deep learning method for surface reconstruction, producing an immersive and navigable 3D mesh for hydrogeological analysis.

</details>


### [73] [Autonomous Driving with Priority-Ordered STL Specifications Under Multimodal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20336)
*Taha Bouzid,Shuhao Qi,Mircea Lazar,Sofie Haesaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种不确定性感知的轨迹规划框架，结合信号时序逻辑（STL）的词典序优先级排序和模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制，在安全关键场景下处理冲突目标并显式考虑多模态不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在安全关键场景中难以同时满足安全、舒适和交通规则等所有需求，且周围交通参与者的轨迹预测存在不确定性，需要一种能显式处理不确定性并对需求进行优先级排序的轨迹规划方法。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的轨迹规划框架，使用词典序优先级排序对STL规范进行层次化约束，在不确定性下保持有效性，并通过MPPI控制实现该规划问题。

Result: 在仿真场景中验证了该方法的有效性，表明框架能够在现实多模态不确定性下高效处理冲突目标。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定性感知轨迹规划框架通过词典序STL优先级与MPPI结合，能够在安全关键场景中有效处理冲突需求并应对多模态不确定性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must plan trajectories that satisfy a multitude of requirements on safety, passenger comfort, and compliance with traffic rules. However, in safety-critical scenarios, it is not always possible to satisfy all requirements simultaneously, necessitating their prioritization based on importance. At the same time, in these safety-critical scenarios, the uncertainty in trajectory predictions of the surrounding traffic, such as other vehicles and pedestrians, should be explicitly accounted for. In this work, we propose an uncertainty-aware trajectory planning framework that incorporates a predefined lexicographic ordering over Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications that stays valid under uncertainty. We implement this formulation with Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control and we demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on simulation scenarios, showing that our framework efficiently handles conflicting objectives under realistic multi-modal uncertainty.

</details>


### [74] [An Infrastructure-less, Control-Independent Solution to Relative Localisation of a Team of Mobile Robots using Ranging Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20365)
*Paolo Golinelli,Tommaso Faraci,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需锚点、完全去中心化的协作定位算法，仅依赖本地里程计、稀疏的机器人间测距和短距离通信，通过多假设贝叶斯框架维护所有可行解，确保在瞬态不可观条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人团队定位方法通常依赖固定基础设施、需要控制机器人运动以保证可观测性，无法满足快速灵活部署和最小化系统需求的实际应用场景，因此需要一种同时解决以上挑战的去中心化协作定位算法。

Method: 采用多假设贝叶斯框架，维护整个可行解集合；仅使用本地里程计、稀疏的机器人间测距和短距离通信；无需锚点和运动控制约束；通过信息共享使每个智能体受益于整个群体的估计，即使在部分连接条件下也能工作。

Result: 算法在无锚点、完全去中心化、无需控制机器人运动保证可观测性的条件下实现了鲁棒的团队定位，在瞬态不可观条件下仍能保持可行性。

Conclusion: 该算法适用于无固定基础设施、需要快速灵活部署且系统需求最小化的机器人团队定位场景，各项所需传感器和通信手段在实际中广泛可用。

Abstract: The ability to localise teams of robots is essential for applications ranging from robotic fleets in unstructured environments to cooperative control and navigation tasks. In such contexts, fixed infrastructure is often unavailable, deployments must be fast and flexible, and system requirements must be minimal. We present a decentralised cooperative localisation algorithm that addresses all these challenges at once. The method is anchor-less, fully decentralised, and, unlike most existing approaches, does not require controlling the robots motion to ensure team observability. It relies only on local odometry, sparse inter-agent ranging measurements, and short-range communication, all of which are widely available in practice. The algorithm adopts a multi-hypothesis Bayesian framework that maintains the entire set of feasible solutions, ensuring robustness under transient unobservable conditions. Moreover, through information sharing, each agent benefits from the estimates of the entire group, even in partially connected conditions.

</details>


### [75] [CoLI: A Reproducible Platform for Continuum Robot Learning via Monolithic 3D Printing and Isomorphic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20389)
*Ziyuan Tang,Chenxi Xiao**

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型开源连续体机器人平台，通过多材料3D打印简化制造流程，利用同构遥操作界面实现无需显式运动学建模的控制，并支持基于模仿学习的自主控制，旨在解决连续体机器人可重复性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人由于制造装配复杂、运动学建模困难及缺乏直观控制界面，导致可重复性差，阻碍了其在研究和实际应用中的推广。

Method: 采用多材料3D打印实现一体式柔性结构制造，提出同构遥操作界面建立直接驱动器级映射以消除运动学建模需求，并在此基础上支持模仿学习的自主控制。

Result: 硬件特性表征和操作任务实验表明，该平台提供了可重复、可学习的连续体机器人系统。

Conclusion: 该开源平台可加速连续体机器人领域的算法开发和系统化基准测试。

Abstract: Continuum robots offer strong potential for manipulation tasks due to their high degrees of freedom, compliant structures, and operational safety. However, their adoption in both research and practical applications has been hindered by reproducibility issues arising from complex fabrication and assembly processes, challenging kinematic modeling, and a lack of intuitive control interfaces. To address these challenges, we present a novel open-source continuum robot design. The platform features a simplified fabrication pipeline enabled by multi-material 3D printing, allowing the arm to be fabricated as a monolithic compliant structure with minimal assembly. Control is achieved through an isomorphic teleoperation interface that establishes a direct actuator-level mapping, eliminating the need for explicit kinematic modeling and providing a singularity-free mapping. Building on this hardware design, the platform further supports imitation-learning-based autonomous control. The proposed system is evaluated through hardware characterization and a set of manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the platform provides a reproducible, learning-ready continuum robot system, accelerating algorithmic development and systematic benchmarking for the continuum robotics community.

</details>


### [76] [LIT-GS: LiDAR-Inertial-Thermal Gaussian Splatting for Illumination-Robust Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20424)
*Shikuan Shi,Chunran Zheng,Jiaming Xu,Tianyong Ye,Tao Yu,Yukang Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIT-GS 提出了一种融合 LiDAR、惯性和热成像的高斯泼溅框架，通过 LiDAR 导出的平面几何约束，在弱光照和纹理缺失场景下显著提升了神经渲染的几何精度与渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiDAR-惯性-视觉 (LIV) 高斯建图管线在光照变化和纹理缺失场景下脆弱，因为其依赖 RGB 光度线索；需要引入热成像与 LiDAR 几何信息来增强鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 利用 LIV 视觉地图点作为置信度感知的跨模态锚点，建立可靠的热-LiDAR 关联；2) 在光束法平差中引入加权 LiDAR 点到面残差，联合优化相机位姿和 3D 点；3) 提出 LiDAR 平面正则化的可微泼溅目标函数，约束渲染 3D 点与局部观测平面对齐。

Result: 在自有序列和公开数据集上，LIT-GS 在几何精度和渲染质量上持续优于基于 LIV 的高斯泼溅基线，尤其在具有挑战性的光照条件下。

Conclusion: 通过注入 LiDAR 平面几何作为显式约束，LIT-GS 有效解决了弱纹理和光照变化场景下的表面增厚与结构漂移问题，提升了高斯泼溅框架的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time neural rendering, yet existing LiDAR-inertial-visual (LIV) Gaussian mapping pipelines remain fragile under illumination changes and texture-deficient scenes due to their reliance on RGB photometric cues. We present LIT-GS, a LiDAR-inertial-thermal Gaussian Splatting framework that injects LiDAR-derived plane geometry as an explicit constraint in both pose/structure refinement and Gaussian optimization. Specifically, we exploit LIV visual map points as confidence-aware cross-modal anchors to establish reliable thermal-LiDAR associations, and incorporate weighted LiDAR point-to-plane residuals into bundle adjustment to jointly refine camera poses and 3D points under weak thermal supervision. Building on the refined structure, we further introduce a LiDAR-plane-regularized differentiable splatting objective that constrains rendered 3D points to align with locally observed planes, mitigating surface thickening and structural drift in low-contrast thermal imagery. Experiments on proprietary sequences and public datasets demonstrate that LIT-GS consistently improves geometric accuracy and rendering quality over state-of-the-art LIV-based Gaussian Splatting baselines, particularly in challenging lighting conditions.

</details>


### [77] [TaCauchy: An Extensible FEM Framework for Vision-Based Tactile Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20426)
*Hengfei Zhao,Yifan Xie,Junhao Gong,Yue Sun,Kai Zhu,Weihua He,Shoujie Li,Haohuan Fu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaCauchy是一个基于有限元法的触觉传感器仿真框架，集成于Isaac Sim平台，利用UIPC求解器直接计算Cauchy应力张量，提供物理真实的力学场仿真，支持多种触觉传感器，在60个并行环境中达到555 FPS吞吐量，物理验证SSIM超过0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉的触觉传感器在强化学习中需要高保真仿真，但现有方法难以在GPU加速的机器人平台上提供准确的机械应力场。

Method: 基于UIPC求解器，从超弹性本构方程直接计算Cauchy应力张量，投影到接触面获得牵引力和压力分布；自动网格生成与几何自适应细化；模块化传感器接口支持多种传感器快速集成。

Result: 单环境33.40 FPS，60个并行环境聚合吞吐555 FPS，应力提取开销低于1 ms；物理验证中力范围1.2556 N至4.7332 N，SSIM高于0.93。

Conclusion: TaCauchy能够为下游机器人操作任务提供准确、基于物理的力监督信号。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors require high-fidelity simulation for reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches struggle to provide accurate mechanical stress fields within GPU-accelerated robotics platforms. We present TaCauchy, an extensible Finite Element Method (FEM) framework that integrates rigorous physics-based force computation into Isaac Sim. Built on the Unified Incremental Potential Contact (UIPC) solver, TaCauchy directly computes Cauchy stress tensors from hyperelastic constitutive laws and projects them onto contact surfaces to obtain traction forces and pressure distributions, providing mechanical ground truth from first principles rather than empirical estimation. Our framework features automatic mesh generation with geometry-aware adaptive refinement and a modular sensor interface enabling rapid integration of diverse sensors (GelSight Mini, DIGIT, 9DTact) with minimal configuration. Performance benchmarks demonstrate 33.40 FPS for single environments and 555 FPS aggregate throughput across 60 parallel environments, with stress extraction overhead under 1 ms. Physical validation experiments show strong agreement between simulated and real tactile responses across force ranges from 1.2556 N to 4.7332 N, achieving SSIM above 0.93, confirming the framework's capability to provide accurate, physically-grounded force supervision for downstream robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [78] [ARC: Adaptive Robust Joint State and Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20428)
*Alexandre Hadji-Thomas,Andrew Stirling,James R. Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的块坐标下降框架，结合自适应鲁棒损失函数、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新和最小加权协方差行列式协方差估计器，实现了在存在异常值的情况下同时估计状态和协方差，无需手动参数调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器测量常受异常值和非高斯噪声污染，导致经典状态估计器产生有偏结果。现有鲁棒估计器能抑制异常值但无法估计测量协方差，而联合状态-协方差估计器假设高斯残差和固定损失形状参数。将两者整合到统一框架中是一个重要机会。

Method: 提出统一的块坐标下降（BCD）框架，包含三个核心组件：1）范数感知的自适应鲁棒损失函数；2）迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）状态更新；3）最小加权协方差行列式（MWCD）协方差估计器。三者交替迭代实现自调优的联合状态-协方差估计。

Result: 在蒙特卡洛仿真和真实超宽带定位实验（杂波非视距环境）中，所提估计器能够一致地恢复真实异常值内点测量协方差，在状态估计精度上达到或超过所有基线方法，且无需手动参数调优。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将鲁棒估计与协方差估计能力统一，实现了自调优的联合状态-协方差估计，在实际应用中无需人工调节参数，具有良好的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Sensor measurements are frequently corrupted by outliers and non-Gaussian noise. These imperfections in the sensor data can cause classical state estimators to generate biased and unreliable state and uncertainty estimates. Robust estimators reject or downweight outliers but do not perform measurement covariance estimation, whereas joint state and covariance estimators assume Gaussian residuals and fixed loss shape parameters. Integrating these two capabilities into a single framework is an opportunity to simultaneously estimate both state and covariance in the presence of outliers. This paper proposes a unified Block-Coordinate Descent framework that combines a norm-aware adaptive robust loss, an Iteratively Reweighted Least-Squares state update, and a Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant covariance estimator, yielding a self-tuning joint state and covariance estimator. The framework is evaluated in a Monte-Carlo simulation and on real-world ultra-wideband localization experiments in cluttered non-line-of-sight environments. Results show that the proposed estimator consistently recovers the true inlier measurement covariance and matches or exceeds the state estimation accuracy of all baselines, without requiring any manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [79] [Slow Brain, Fast Planner: Latency-Resilient VLM-Augmented Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20458)
*Zhenghao "Mark'' Peng,Honglin He,Quanyi Li,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对移动机器人在人行道导航中存在的轨迹评分差距问题（规划器首选轨迹并非最优），本文提出了一种VLM-规划器接口，利用视觉语言模型从规划器生成的候选轨迹集中选择最佳索引，并通过几何相似性与指数衰减的轨迹级融合层，将VLM的延迟选择实时融合到规划器评分中，在无需额外训练的情况下显著降低了平均位移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的锚点式规划器虽能实时生成多样化的候选轨迹，但其评分函数在复杂场景下常无法选出最优轨迹，导致机器人驶入草地、冲向行人或方向错误，即存在轨迹评分差距。作者认为根本原因在于规划器缺乏高层场景理解能力。

Method: 提出VLM-Planner接口：1）利用VLM从规划器候选轨迹集中选择最佳索引；2）设计无需训练、对延迟鲁棒的轨迹级融合层，通过几何相似性与指数衰减机制将过时的VLM选择结果实时融合到规划器评分中，以适配5-20Hz的控制频率。

Result: 在约2000个挑战性真实场景中，VLM选择相比规划器最佳选择实现了30%的ADE降低；在仿真中，评分融合在高达5秒的延迟下仍保持超过80%的成功率；完整系统在校园人行道上验证有效。

Conclusion: VLM与基于学习的规划器结合能够有效弥补轨迹评分差距，提升复杂场景下的导航性能，且提出的融合层无需训练即可应对VLM的延迟问题，为将大型视觉语言模型集成到实时机器人系统中提供了一种实用的接口方案。

Abstract: Learning-based planners for sidewalk navigation can generate diverse candidate trajectories in real time, yet their scoring functions often fail to select the best trajectory in challenging situations, outputting trajectories that make the mobile robot drive onto grass, toward pedestrians, or in the wrong direction, even when better candidates exist in the same set. We call this the trajectory scoring gap: in real-world sidewalk navigation, the gap between an anchor-based planner's top choice and the best possible candidate is substantial, likely due to limited high-level scene understanding capability of the planner. Rather than replacing the planner with an end-to-end Vision-Language-Action model, we propose a VLM-Planner interface that uses a VLM to select a candidate index from the planner's proposal set and then fuse it with the planner's initial output. However, VLMs take 1--3s per query and so cannot directly drive a 5--20Hz control loop. We contribute a training-free, latency-resilient trajectory-level fusion layer that turns a stale VLM selection into real-time planner scoring via geometric similarity with exponential decay. On $\sim$2,000 challenging real-world scenarios (e.g., junctions, pedestrian encounters), VLM selection achieves 30% ADE reduction versus the planner's best selection, while the planner remains competitive in routine situations. In simulation, Score Fusion maintains >80% success rate with delays up to 5s. We demonstrate the full system on a mobile robot navigating challenging campus sidewalks with varied network latency.

</details>


### [80] [GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20479)
*Nastaran Darabi,Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 GroundControl，一种基于轨迹一致性的不确定性估计方法，通过卡尔曼滤波建模距离-目标动态的统计偏差，并在选择性风险-覆盖协议（SRCN）下显著优于熵、共形和启发式基线，实现视觉-语言导航代理中可解释的失败预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航代理在基准任务上表现尚可，但常因振荡、停滞或低效绕路等轨迹级故障而失败。可靠部署需要能够在执行过程中预测新兴失败动态的不确定性信号，而非仅反映瞬时动作熵。

Method: 提出 GroundControl，定义为对名义目标导向的距目标距离动态的统计偏差。使用常速卡尔曼滤波器建模距离演化，结合归一化创新统计量与轨迹特征（进度、单调性、路径效率、振荡行为），得到反映几何和时间不一致性的不确定性分数。同时形式化选择性风险-覆盖导航（SRCN）协议评估不确定性排序质量。

Result: 在五个EB-Navigation子集（N=300）上，轨迹一致性不确定性在基于成功的选择性风险下达到接近oracle的排序性能，GPT-4o模型的加权平均E-AURC_SR=0.0024，显著优于熵、共形和启发式基线。在SPL-based选择性评估下，GroundControl在所有模型和子集上均实现最低AURC和E-AURC。

Conclusion: 建模与目标导向动态的偏差为预测视觉-语言导航代理的失败提供了可解释且鲁棒的信号，轨迹一致性不确定性是比局部预测分散更有效的失败预警指标。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation agents achieve competitive average success on benchmark tasks, yet failures often arise through predictable trajectory-level breakdowns such as oscillation, stagnation, or inefficient detours. Reliable deployment, therefore, requires uncertainty signals that anticipate emerging failure dynamics during execution rather than reflect only instantaneous action entropy. We introduce \emph{GroundControl}, a trajectory-consistent uncertainty estimator defined as statistical deviation from nominal goal-directed distance-to-goal dynamics aggregated over an episode. GroundControl models distance evolution using a constant-velocity Kalman filter and combines normalized innovation statistics with complementary trajectory features capturing progress, monotonicity, path efficiency, and oscillatory behavior. The resulting uncertainty score reflects geometric and temporal inconsistency in navigation behavior rather than local prediction dispersion. To evaluate uncertainty quality independently of task success, we formalize \emph{Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation (SRCN)}, a protocol that measures how effectively an uncertainty score ranks episodes by failure or inefficiency using risk--coverage curves and AURC / E-AURC summaries. Across five EB-Navigation splits ($N=300$ episodes), trajectory-consistent uncertainty achieves near-oracle ordering under success-based selective risk, with weighted-average $\mathrm{E\text{-}AURC}_{\mathrm{SR}}=0.0024$ for the GPT-4o model, substantially outperforming entropy-, conformal-, and heuristic baselines. Under SPL-based selective evaluation, GroundControl consistently achieves the lowest AURC and E-AURC across models and navigation splits. These results show that modeling deviation from goal-directed dynamics provides an interpretable and robust signal for anticipating navigation failures in vision-language agents.

</details>


### [81] [Fast Human Attention Prediction for Fixation-guided Active Perception in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20491)
*Fatma Youssef Mohammed,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GazeLNN，一种基于液体神经网络和MobileNetV3的轻量级扫描路径预测模型，在MIT Low Resolution数据集上以0.61 GFLOPs实现SOTA性能（ScanMatch分数0.47），计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升6倍，并成功集成到无人机自主导航的主动视觉控制策略中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉注意力通过结构化扫描路径高效处理场景，但将这一行为注入机器人自主系统尚处于初期阶段，且现有预测模型计算成本过高，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出GazeLNN架构，以液体神经网络（LNN）作为递归引擎，MobileNetV3进行特征提取，采用自回归方式预测时序注视热图，条件为当前视觉刺激和注视历史。

Result: 仅需0.61 GFLOPs，在MIT Low Resolution数据集上达到0.47 ScanMatch分数；计算成本降低99.40%，推理加速最高达6倍；成功将模型集成到基于强化学习的主动相机-机器人控制策略中，在真实无人机平台上验证了自主导航能力。

Conclusion: GazeLNN展示了高效的扫描路径预测能力，且其超低计算开销使其能够有效应用于机器人自主系统中的注意力引导感知，填补了人类注意力建模与机器人自主性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Human visual attention relies on structured scanpaths to efficiently process scenes, yet instilling this behavior into robot autonomy is in its infancy and hindered by the high,computational costs of existing predictive models. To address this, we introduce GazeLNN, a computationally lightweight,scanpath prediction model that leverages Liquid Neural Networks as its recurrent engine and employs MobileNetV3 for feature extraction. Operating auto-regressively, the architecture predicts sequential fixation heatmaps conditioned on the current visual stimulus and fixation history. Despite requiring only 0.61 GFLOPs, GazeLNN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MIT Low Resolution dataset achieving 0.47 ScanMatch score. It outperforms existing recurrent baselines across diverse evaluation metrics, while reducing computational costs by 99.40% and accelerating inference by up to six times. To investigate the role of human attention modeling in robot autonomy and demonstrate the practical utility of this highly efficient architecture, we integrate GazeLNN into an active camera-robot control policy trained via Reinforcement Learning. This integration enables human-fixation-guided perception during autonomous navigation, validated through successful real-world deployments on an aerial robot.

</details>


### [82] [Increasing Resilience of Continuum Robots via Motion Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20495)
*Oxana Shamilyan,Ievgen Kabin,Zoya Dyka,Oleksandr Sudakov,Peter Langendoerfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了弹性连续体机器人的运动规划，通过将层次分析法（AHP）与遗传算法和A*算法结合，基于距离、电机损伤、机械损伤和精度四个准则进行多准则决策路径规划。结果表明，遗传算法性能不受环境基数影响，能生成更多样化路径，提升机器人弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了延长连续体机器人的维护周期，需要提升其弹性（resilience）。现有路径规划算法通常只考虑单一目标（如最短路径），忽略了机器人机械系统和电机的长期损耗。本文旨在通过多准则决策方法，在路径规划中同时考虑距离、电机损伤、机械损伤和精度四个因素，以生成更有利于机器人长期运行的路径。

Method: 采用两种经典路径规划算法（遗传算法和A*算法）作为基础，引入层次分析法（AHP）作为多准则决策工具，对生成的路径基于四个准则（距离、电机损伤、机械损伤、精度）进行质量评估。实验在两种模拟环境中进行：一种包含单路径点和多路径点，另一种仅包含多路径点。环境部分特征基于真实机器人原型实现。

Result: 与A*算法相比，遗传算法的性能时间不受环境基数（即路径点数量）的影响。遗传算法能够生成更加多样化的路径，从而提升连续体机器人的弹性。

Conclusion: 在弹性连续体机器人的运动规划中，基于遗传算法结合AHP的多准则路径规划方法优于A*结合AHP的方法，因其对环境复杂性不敏感且能生成多样化路径，有利于提高机器人的弹性和延长维护周期。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental study of motion planning for resilient continuum robots. In this study we mainly focused on multi-criteria decision-making, its application for path-planning algorithms, impact on the generated path and execution time. To do this, we used two well-known algorithms for path planning, namely Genetic algorithm and A star algorithm, and modified them by adding the Analytical Hierarchy Process algorithm to evaluate the quality of the paths generated. In our experiment the Analytical Hierarchy Process considers four different criteria, i.e. distance, motors damage, mechanical damage of the robot's arm and accuracy, each considered to contribute to the resilience of a continuum robot. The use of different criteria is necessary to increase the time to maintenance operations of the continuum robot. We conducted the experiments using two different simulated environments of the robot. Although we significantly simplified the robot's model and its environment, we still implemented some of the features of the environment based on the real robot prototype. In particular, one of the environments has single- as well as multi-path points, and other consists of the multi-path points only. The results show that, in contrast to A star, the performance time of Genetic algorithm does not depend on the environment's cardinality. It generates more diverse paths, which increases the robot's resilience.

</details>


### [83] [Generating Robot Hands from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20549)
*Sha Yi,Nicklas Hansen,Xueqian Bai,Carmelo Sferrazza,Michael T. Tolley,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个数据驱动框架，利用超过400万帧人类手指运动数据，通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置来优化机器人手部设计，生成6自由度通用手和低自由度专用手，并训练强化学习智能体加速设计搜索，实验证明该手部在遥操作指尖追踪上优于现有商业产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习在控制方面进展迅速，但学习机器人的物理身体仍然困难，因为联合搜索设计与控制会形成非常大的组合问题。

Method: 利用人类日常操作的400多万帧手指运动数据，通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置来优化树状结构机器人手部设计；并训练强化学习智能体提出好的手部设计和关节角度，将搜索时间从数小时缩短至数分钟。

Result: 6自由度通用手在遥操作指尖追踪上优于可用的商用机器人手部；3自由度专用手能以降低的机械复杂性重现结构化的类人及合成轨迹。

Conclusion: 大规模人类运动数据不仅可用于训练机器人控制器，也可作为优化和生成机器人物理形态的参考。

Abstract: Robot learning has advanced rapidly in learning control, but learning the physical body of a robot remains much more difficult because jointly searching over design and control creates a very large combinatorial problem. Here, we present a data-driven framework for generating robot hands from human demonstrations. Instead of learning a complex controller together with each candidate design, we generate robot hand designs using the same simple control policy used after fabrication: matching fingertip positions through inverse kinematics. Using more than 4 million frames of human fingertip motion from everyday manipulation, our algorithm optimizes tree-structured robot hands to reproduce desired target motions. The framework produced both a 6-degree-of-freedom (DoF) general-purpose hand and lower-DoF task-specific hands with spatial four-bar mimic joints. To accelerate the search over designs, we trained a reinforcement-learning (RL) actor to propose good hand designs and joint angles, reducing search time from hours to minutes. We fabricated the mechanisms directly as one-piece articulated structures with print-in-place joints. In real-world experiments, the 6-DoF hand achieved highly accurate teleoperated fingertip tracking better than available commercial robot hands, whereas the specialized 3-DoF hands reproduced structured human and synthetic trajectories with reduced mechanical complexity. These results showed that large-scale human motion data can be used not only to train robot controllers but also as a reference for optimizing and generating the physical embodiment of robots.

</details>


### [84] [MemoryWAM: Efficient World Action Modeling with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20562)
*Sizhe Yang,Juncheng Mu,Tianming Wei,Chenhao Lu,Xiaofan Li,Linning Xu,Zhengrong Xue,Zhecheng Yuan,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoryWAM提出了一种混合记忆设计的世界动作模型，通过结合近期帧、事件边界锚定帧和压缩要点令牌，实现了高效的长序列记忆建模，在长时域、记忆依赖的机器人操作任务中超越现有VLA和WAM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在推理效率与长程记忆能力之间存在根本性权衡：基于有限窗口的方法在非马尔可夫环境中表现不佳，而保持完整历史的方法则时间和空间成本随序列长度线性增长。

Method: MemoryWAM采用混合记忆架构，包含三种记忆组件：（1）近期帧提供细粒度短期上下文；（2）事件边界锚定帧捕获关键状态变化；（3）压缩要点令牌总结远程历史。通过定制化注意力机制，同时检索详细短期上下文和压缩长期上下文。

Result: 在长时域、记忆依赖的机器人操作任务（仿真和真实世界）中，MemoryWAM在保持良好计算效率的同时，优于强VLA和WAM基线方法。

Conclusion: 混合记忆设计是解决WAM中推理效率与长程记忆权衡问题的有效方案，能够支持记忆依赖的决策制定，同时降低推理延迟和GPU内存使用。

Abstract: Robust robotic manipulation in the real world requires not only an understanding of the current observation, but also memory and dynamics modeling. World action models (WAMs) possess these capabilities by jointly modeling visual foresight and actions conditioned on both current and historical observations, making them a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing WAMs face a fundamental trade-off: methods with efficient inference typically condition only on a bounded window of recent observations and therefore struggle in non-Markovian environments, whereas methods that preserve long histories incur time and space costs that grow substantially with sequence length. To address this challenge, we introduce MemoryWAM, a world action model with efficient persistent memory. MemoryWAM uses a hybrid memory design that combines recent frames, event-boundary anchor frames, and compact gist tokens that summarize long-range history. A tailored attention mechanism enables retrieval of both detailed short-term context and compressed long-term context, supporting memory-dependent decision-making with reduced inference latency and GPU memory usage. Across long-horizon, memory-dependent manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world, MemoryWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines while maintaining favorable computational efficiency.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Hopfological algebra, revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19485)
*Juan Omar Gómez,Gustavo Jasso,Marius Nielsen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个$\infty$-范畴化的方法来研究Khovanov–Qi的Hopfological代数，通过在幺半$\infty$-范畴的模$\infty$-范畴中重新构造先前理论，从而在多个基础层面改进了该理论，并进一步将该方法推广到任意刚性紧生成对称幺半稳定$\infty$-范畴上。附录中还比较了Hopfological导出范畴与Holm–Jørgensen的$Q$-形导出范畴的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Khovanov–Qi的Hopfological代数提供一个更精细、更基础的$\infty$-范畴化框架，并探索其在更一般的对称幺半稳定$\infty$-范畴中的推广。

Method: 采用$\infty$-范畴论方法，将Hopfological代数的构造重新表述为幺半$\infty$-范畴中的模$\infty$-范畴，并推广到刚性紧生成对称幺半稳定$\infty$-范畴上。

Result: 得到了Hopfological代数更一般的变体，并将其与Holm–Jørgensen的$Q$-形导出范畴进行了比较。

Conclusion: $\infty$-范畴化视角不仅改进了原有Hopfological代数理论的基础，还将其推广到更广泛的设定中，并建立了与$Q$-形导出范畴的联系。

Abstract: We propose an $\infty$-categorical approach to Khovanov--Qi's Hopfological algebra that, in particular, refines several foundational aspects of the theory by recasting the previous constructions in terms of $\infty$-categories of modules in monoidal $\infty$-categories. This perspective leads to a more general variant of Hopfological algebra that takes place over an arbitrary rigidly-compactly generated symmetric monoidal stable $\infty$-category, which we also outline in the article. In the appendix, we compare the construction of Hopfological derived categories to that of Holm--Jørgensen's $Q$-shaped derived categories.

</details>


### [86] [Convolution algebras associated to representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19783)
*Dragos Crisan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究复约化群G的表示V和Borel-稳定子空间M上的Steinberg型簇Z，证明了在“可粘合性”条件下，Z的等变Borel-Moore同调或K-理论（配备卷积积）可视为两个nil-Hecke代数在其局部化中的交集，并给出了这些新代数的极点和留数描述。结果推广了仿射Hecke代数、DAHA以及Coulomb分支的经典结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将仿射Hecke代数、双仿射Hecke代数（DAHA）和Coulomb分支等经典代数的几何构造统一到更一般的框架下，通过Steinberg型簇的等变同调/ K-理论来刻画。

Method: 利用“可粘合性”条件，将两个nil-Hecke代数的局部化进行交集运算，得到卷积代数的几何实现；同时使用极点和留数方法描述新代数结构。对G及其环路群情形分别处理。

Result: 在可粘合性条件下，Z的等变Borel-Moore同调（或K-理论）的卷积代数同构于两个nil-Hecke代数局部化的交集，并可通过极点和留数显式刻画。

Conclusion: 该框架统一推广了Ginzburg–Kapranov–Vasserot的仿射Hecke/DAHA几何构造以及Teleman和Gannon–Webster的Coulomb分支粘合构造。

Abstract: Given a complex reductive group $G$, a representation $V$ of $G$ and a Borel-stable subspace $M \subset V$, we consider the associated Steinberg-type variety $Z$. We prove that, under a certain condition on $(V,M)$, called gluability, the equivariant Borel-Moore homology or $K$-theory of $Z$, equipped with the convolution product, is obtained as the intersection of two copies of the nil-Hecke algebra inside its localization. We also provide a description of these new algebras in terms of poles and residues. Similar results are obtained when $G$ is replaced by its loop group. This generalizes results of Ginzburg, Kapranov and Vasserot describing the affine Hecke algebra and DAHA, as well as a result of Teleman and Gannon--Webster that realizes certain Coulomb branches by gluing two copies of the universal centralizer.

</details>


### [87] [Stiefel-Whitney classes for symmetric groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19843)
*Sujeet Bhalerao,Jyotirmoy Ganguly,Steven Spallone*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对称群$S_n$表示$\pi$的Stiefel-Whitney类$w_k(\pi)$的若干结果：每个SWC是$\pi$在对合元上的特征值多项式；对固定$k$，当$n\to\infty$时，$w_k(\pi)=0$的不可约表示比例趋近100%；给出了首个非零SWC的简单判别准则；显式计算了前四个SWC；并给出了交错群的类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称群表示的Stiefel-Whitney类的代数性质与渐近行为，填补该领域在组合表示论中的理论空白。

Method: 利用特征值多项式与对合元的关系，结合组合渐近分析，导出SWC的零化比例与判别准则。

Result: SWC由对合元上的特征值多项式决定；固定$k$时几乎全部不可约表示的SWC为零；显式前四个SWC公式；推广至交错群。

Conclusion: 对称群表示的SWC具有高度退化性（渐近零化），且可通过简单的代数准则判定其非零性，成果可自然推广至交错群。

Abstract: We prove several results about Stiefel-Whitney Classes (SWCs) $w_k(π)$ of representations $π$ of $S_n$. First, each SWC is polynomial in the character values of $π$ at involutions. Next, for a fixed $k$, the proportion of irreducible $π$ for which $w_k(π)=0$ approaches $100\%$ as $n \to \infty$. A similar result holds for the top SWCs. We also provide a simple criterion which determines the first nonvanishing SWC for a representation. The first four SWCs are computed explicitly. Finally, we give analogues for alternating groups.

*Souvik Pal,Supravat Sarkar,G. Krishna Teja*

TL;DR: 本文研究了Borcherds-Kac-Moody Lie代数中一类新的非标准可积最高权模（对应新的符号支配积分锥P^±），在秩2情形下分析了Verma覆盖中的模结构和极大向量，探索了Kac-Kazhdan下界的严格性，并在Kac-Kazhdan方程在根锥内部有唯一解时给出了所有模的表示和特征标。

Conclusion: 本文开启了对于P^±锥中权重所对应的非标准可积单最高权模的系统研究，秩2情形的结论为后续高秩推广奠定了基础，并展示了这类模与经典可积模在结构上的本质差异。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{g}(A)$ be the Borcherds-Kac-Moody Lie algebra (BKM LA), corresponding to a BKM Cartan matrix $A$ filled by negative integers. Let $P^+\subset \mathfrak{h}^*$ the classical dominant integral cone (wherein pairings are non-negative). The non-integrable simple highest weight modules $L(μ)$'s widely studied were broadly those by Naito ([Trans. Amer. Soc., 1995]), for $μ$'s dot-linked to $P^+$-translates of sums $- \sum_{j\in J}α_j$ of mutually orthogonal and imaginary simple roots $α_j$'s.
  Recently, we computed weights of all highest weight $\mathfrak{g}$-modules $V$'s, and characters of $L(ρ)$ for Weyl vector $ρ$ in negative type-$A$. These needed a family of ``integrable'' $L(μ)$'s for $μ$'s inside our novel signed-dominant-integral cone $P^{\pm}$ (which generalizes $P^+$). Pairings $μ(α_i^{\vee})\leq 0$ therein are multiples of $\frac{A_{ii}}{2}$ for all $i$. Nevertheless, $L(μ)$ contain ``Chevalley-Serre relations'' $f_i^{\frac{2}{A_{ii}}{μ(α_i^{\vee})}+1}L(μ)_μ=0$; which differ from relations in $L(λ)$ for all $λ\in P^+$, and are seemingly unstudied earlier (also by Naito).
  This paper initiates the study in rank-2, of the module structures and maximal vectors (or Verma embeddings) in the Verma covers $M(μ)$ of $L(μ)$'s for $μ\in P^{\pm}$. In this, our goal is to explore in weight spaces of those Verma covers, the strictness (or otherwise, an uniform equality) of lower bounds by Kac and Kazhdan ([Adv. Math., 1979]) for count of linearly independent maximal vectors. We obtain presentations and characters of all $V$'s when Kac-Kazhdan equation has unique solution in the interior of root-cone. This builds on the unique solution case in Lemma 3.1 from that paper.

</details>


### [89] [Silting t-structures in $Q$-shaped derived categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20447)
*Anastasios Slaftsos*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文利用Saorín-Šťovíček的对应关系，在Q-形导出范畴中构造了一族由Q的 admissible 划分诱导的t-结构，证明了这些t-结构由silting对象诱导，并给出了相应的同调刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-结构在三角范畴研究中具有核心地位，而由silting/tilting对象诱导的t-结构具有优良性质并与导出等价紧密相关。本文旨在Q-形导出范畴中构造具有类似性质的t-结构族，并探索其组合刻画。

Method: 利用Saorín-Šťovíček建立的Frobenius正合范畴中的余遗传余挠对与其稳定范畴中t-结构之间的对应关系，在Q-形导出范畴中通过Q的admissible划分构造t-结构；在双纤维对象的Frobenius正合范畴中显式描述相应的余挠对，并用同调消失条件刻画对应的co-aisle。

Result: 构造了一族由Q的admissible划分诱导的t-结构，证明了它们由silting对象诱导（该对象可由Q的组合完全确定），并在某些组合条件失效的情形（如循环箭图）下证明该范畴可能无非平凡t-结构。

Conclusion: 本文在Q-形导出范畴中建立了t-结构与Q的组合结构之间的深刻联系，推广了已知的导出等价，同时揭示了类似于Linckelmann在稳定模范畴中观察到的现象。

Abstract: Torsion pairs, and in particular t-structures, play a central role in the study of triangulated categories. Specifically, t-structures induced by silting (or tilting) objects often admit desirable properties with strong connections to derived equivalences. In this paper, using the correspondence of Saorín-Šťovíček between cohereditary cotorsion pairs in Frobenius exact categories and t-structures in their stable categories, we construct a family of t-structures in the $Q$-shaped derived category of Holm and Jorgensen, arising from admissible partitions of $Q$. We give an explicit description of the associated cotorsion pairs inside the Frobenius exact category of the bifibrant objects, and we identify the corresponding co-aisles by certain homological vanishing conditions. Such t-structures are proved to be induced by a silting object, that can be completely determined by the combinatorics of $Q$. Finally, we illustrate our results by recovering well-known equivalences in the $Q$-shaped setting, while also providing examples where the combinatorial conditions fail (e.g. cyclic quivers), showing that such categories may admit no non-trivial t-structures, revealing phenomena analogous to those observed by Linckelmann in stable module categories.

</details>

Apart from studying the beam based physics, the large far detector is also capable of studying various other physics cases involving solar, atmopsheric and supernova neutrinos. Under the ESSnuSBplus project, there will be a low energy monitored beam and a low energy nuSTORM facility for the measurement of cross-section.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究粒子物理学中大型模型的神经缩放定律，探索物理领域独特的低成本模拟数据是否允许偏向数据驱动而非参数驱动的缩放模式。

Method: 在高能物理喷注分类任务上，通过引入更多样化且与下游任务对齐的预训练数据，工程化缩放行为使其倾向于需要更多数据而非更大模型。

Result: 粒子物理模型的性能同样遵循幂律缩放；利用物理领域低成本高保真模拟数据的优势，可以通过数据工程实现偏向数据扩展的缩放模式。

Conclusion: 粒子物理领域独特的低成本模拟数据优势允许工程化缩放行为，偏向数据扩展而非模型扩展，这为资源分配提供了新指导。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe how model performance improves as a power law in compute, model size, and dataset size. While well-established for large language models, these relationships are emerging for large models in particle physics. As with language, empirical studies show that the performance scales as a power law. However, unlike natural language or image domains, fundamental physics has high-fidelity simulators that produce synthetic data cheaply. This favors scaling regimes where additional data is cheaper than additional parameters, and allows the pretraining dataset itself to be engineered to influence the scaling. For the task of classifying hadronic jets produced in collisions of high-energy particle beams, we show that the scaling behavior can be engineered towards requiring more data rather than larger models by inclusion of pretraining data which is more diverse and better aligned with the downstream classification task.

</details>

Conclusion: 该外差上转换方案能显著提升低质量轴子搜索灵敏度，为未来实验提供了理论框架和优化设计指导。

Abstract: We study resonant heterodyne up-conversion in the RADES-BabyIAXO haloscope as a method to search for low-mass dark matter axions using microwave cavities. Starting from axion electrodynamics, we derive the axion-induced source term and the power extracted through a readout mode, explicitly accounting for the finite axion linewidth. This leads to effective quality factors that determine the pump-axion mixing, detection bandwidth, and detected signal power. We extend the BI-RME 3D full-wave formulation to heterodyne axion detection in a realistic two-port cavity, including pump leakage into the readout channel. Applying the formalism to the largest RADES-BabyIAXO cavity identifies the $\mathrm{quasi\textrm{-}TE}_{011}-\mathrm{quasi\textrm{-}TM}_{010}$ mode pair as a favorable configuration, enabling sensitivity to axion frequencies between 0.9 and 34.6 MHz. Analytical and full-wave predictions show excellent agreement at resonance, while the full-wave model provides a more accurate description off resonance and allows a precise characterization of the pump leakage. We also derive the optimal port couplings that maximize the scanning rate. Sensitivity projections for cryogenic copper and superconducting niobium cavities indicate that, under thermal-noise-limited conditions and assuming sufficient pump-leakage rejection, the experiment could probe axion-photon couplings down to $10^{-15}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ at 90% confidence level, representing a significant improvement over previous heterodyne-based searches.

</details>


### [93] [HEPTv2: End-to-End Efficient Point Transformer for Charged Particle Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20437)
*Siqi Miao,Shitij Govil,Jack P. Rodgers,Mia Liu,Javier Duarte,Shih-Chieh Hsu,Yuan-Tang Chou,Pan Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: HEPTv2是一种端到端的点变换器架构，用于高能物理中的带电粒子径迹重建，无需图构建或聚类。在TrackML数据集上实现了98.6%的双多数追踪效率和0.8%的假阳性率，推理时间约15ms，峰值内存0.4GB，相比现有方法在精度-延迟权衡上达到新的最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）面临前所未有的对撞密度，需要精确高效的径迹重建。现有图神经网络方法在图构建和处理上成本高昂，而基于变换器的方法依赖辅助阶段阻碍端到端优化。

Method: HEPTv2采用端到端点变换器架构，包含局部感知点编码器和径迹解码器。编码器在探测器坐标空间中使用局部敏感哈希保持几何信息并实现高效局部注意力；解码器通过分扇区解码和直接命中到径迹预测解决歧义，在联合编解码器监督下实现全流水线端到端优化。

Result: 在TrackML上，HEPTv2达到98.6%双多数追踪效率和0.8%假阳性率，推理时间约15ms，峰值内存0.4GB。相比最强现有变换器效率提升4.5%，相比优化的图流水线效率提升1.1-2.2%，延迟分别降低7倍和38-52倍。延迟和内存随命中数近似线性扩展（支持高达5×10^5个命中）。

Conclusion: 结果表明端到端变换器能够提供HL-LHC实时粒子重建所需的精度和效率，确立了精度-延迟权衡的新最先进水平。

Abstract: Charged-particle tracking -- reconstructing trajectories from sparse detector measurements -- is a fundamental high-energy-physics inference problem and a canonical example of learning under extreme combinatorial ambiguity. At the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC), tracking must remain accurate and efficient despite unprecedented collision densities. Graph neural networks perform strongly, but incur substantial costs from graph construction and processing, while transformer-based approaches rely on auxiliary stages that prevent end-to-end optimization. To address this, we present HEPTv2, an end-to-end point-transformer architecture that reconstructs tracks from detector hits in one trainable pipeline. HEPTv2 combines a locality-aware point encoder with a track decoder that predicts complete trajectories without graph-building, clustering, or filtering. The encoder uses locality-sensitive hashing in detector coordinate space to preserve tracking-relevant geometry while enabling efficient local attention. The decoder resolves ambiguities through sectorized decoding and direct hit-to-track prediction under joint encoder-decoder supervision, allowing the full pipeline to be optimized end-to-end. On TrackML, HEPTv2 achieves 98.6% double-majority tracking efficiency at a 0.8% fake rate, while requiring only $\sim$15~ms inference time and 0.4~GB peak memory per event on a NVIDIA A100 GPU. Latency and memory scale approximately linearly for events with up to $5\times10^5$ hits. HEPTv2 establishes a new state of the art in the accuracy-latency trade-off, improving efficiency by 4.5% over the strongest prior transformer and by 1.1--2.2% over optimized graph-based pipelines, while reducing latency by factors of 7 and 38--52, respectively. These results show end-to-end transformers can deliver the accuracy and efficiency required for real-time particle reconstruction at the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [94] [Observation of electroweak production of pairs of Z bosons in proton-proton collisions at 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20558)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验首次报道了在222.3 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据中，电弱产生的一对Z玻色子与两个喷注（ZZjj → ℓℓννjj）的证据。在 fiducial 体积内测得电弱产生截面为0.37⁺⁰·¹⁴₋₀.₁₂(stat)⁺⁰·⁰⁶₋₀.₀₆(syst) fb，与标准模型预言0.39±0.06 fb一致。信号显著度为3.1标准差（预期2.8标准差），与四轻子道合并后显著度达5.0标准差（预期4.5标准差），并给出了反常四重规范耦合的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型中电弱产生ZZjj过程的理论预言，并寻找超出标准模型的反常四重规范耦合信号。

Method: 基于CMS探测器在√s=13 TeV、积分亮度138 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据，选择具有两个同味异号轻子、大的缺失横动量以及大快度间隔和大不变质量的两个喷注的事件，通过计数实验测量电弱产生截面，并与标准模型预测比较。

Result: 电弱产生ZZjj过程的 fiducial 截面测得为0.37⁺⁰·¹⁴₋₀.₁₂(stat)⁺⁰·⁰⁶₋₀.₀₆(syst) fb，信号显著度3.1σ（预期2.8σ）；与四轻子道合并后显著度达5.0σ（预期4.5σ）；对反常四重规范耦合给出了基于维度-8有效场论算符的限制。

Conclusion: 首次观察到电弱产生ZZjj过程的证据，测量结果与标准模型预言一致；通过与四轻子道组合，实现了对电弱产生Z玻色子对的5.0σ观测，确立了该过程的观测证据；同时给出了反常四重规范耦合的约束。

Abstract: The first evidence of electroweak (EW) production of pairs of Z bosons in association with two jets (jj) in the final state ZZjj $\to$ $\ell\ellνν$jj, where $\ell$ = e, $μ$, is reported by the CMS experiment. The analysis is based on a data sample of proton-proton (pp) collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Events are selected by requiring exactly two leptons of same flavor and opposite charge, large missing transverse momentum, and two jets with a large rapidity separation and large invariant mass. The EW production cross section in a fiducial volume is $σ_{\mathrm{EW}}$(pp $\to$ ZZjj $\to$ $\ell\ellνν$jj) = 0.37$^{+0.14}_{-0.12}$ (stat)$^{+0.06}_{-0.06}$ (syst) fb, in agreement with the standard model prediction of 0.39 $\pm$ 0.06 fb. The observed (expected) significance of the signal is 3.1 (2.8) standard deviations. Limits on anomalous quartic gauge couplings are derived in terms of dimension-8 effective field theory operators. A combination with the previously reported result from the ZZ decay channel with four charged leptons yields an observed (expected) significance of 5.0 (4.5) standard deviations for the EW production of Z boson pairs.

</details>

*Andrea Fontana,Simone Tambascia,Ciro Di Carluccio,Andrea Esposito,Bernardo Spagnolo,Andrea M. Chiariello*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子Game of Life元胞自动机模型模拟2025年夏季意大利西尼罗病毒疫情传播，通过优化蚊虫出生率和移除率参数，实现了对局部和区域平均累计感染病例的高精度拟合，并量化了蚊虫控制措施及气候生态变化对病毒扩散的影响。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来意大利西尼罗病毒传播异常升高，特别是在拉齐奥南部、坎帕尼亚和威尼托地区出现高感染峰值，亟需有效的建模工具来理解疫情传播机制并支持决策。

Method: 采用量子版本的Game of Life元胞自动机模型，其中人类动态遵循GOL规则，而疾病载体（蚊虫）的随机动态及其与人类的相互作用同时进行，仅优化蚊虫出生率和移除率两个参数。

Result: 模型在局部和区域平均层面均能高精度拟合累计感染病例曲线；通过改变参数值可量化蚊虫防控措施效果以及气候生态变化导致的蚊虫激增影响。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了意大利西尼罗病毒感染的通用定量描述，可作为测试不同环境场景的支持工具，有助于决策者制定监测病媒动态和控制病毒扩散的策略。

Abstract: In the last years, an anomalously high spreading of West Nile virus (WNV) has been observed in Italy, with particularly high peaks of infections in southern Lazio, Campania and Veneto regions. The main disease vector for WNV is represented by Culex pipiens mosquitoes, which spread human infections through their bites. Here, we investigate WNV fever epidemic diffusion during summer season 2025 in Italy through a computational approach based on a quantum version of the Game of Life (GOL) cellular automaton model. Specifically, human dynamics evolves according to the GOL rules, while stochastic dynamics of disease vectors, i.e., mosquitoes, as well as their interaction with humans, simultaneously occur. We show that this model fits the curves of cumulative infected individuals with high accuracy, either at local and average-regional level, with only optimization of mosquito birth and removal rates parameters. Furthermore, leveraging model flexibility, we show that changes in model parameters values elucidate system response to environmental variations. For instance, we quantify, e.g., the impact of mosquito spreading containment measures or sudden mosquito increasing abundance due to climatic and ecological changes. Overall, we provide a general, quantitative description of WNV infection spreading in Italy which could represent a supportive tool to test different environmental scenarios and could be useful to devise strategies for decision makers to monitor disease vector dynamics and to control consequent virus diffusion.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [A land of monotone plenty, bis repetita: from classical to weak optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19516)
*Virginie Ehrlacher,Rodrigue Lelotte,Luca Nenna*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了经典最优输运中的c-循环单调性本质上等价于零阶最优性条件（即输运代价泛函在径向锥上的非负性），并将这一视角推广到弱最优输运问题，此时对应一阶最优性条件。在适当假设下，该性质刻画了最优输运计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为c-循环单调性这一经典概念提供新的理解视角，并将其推广到弱最优输运问题中，统一两类输运问题的最优性条件刻画。

Method: 将c-循环单调性重新解释为输运代价泛函在径向锥上的非负性（零阶条件），进而将这一观点推广到弱最优输运，得到其一阶线性化条件。通过这种统一的变分视角，证明该性质在适当假设下刻画最优输运计划。

Result: 建立了经典最优输运中c-循环单调性与零阶最优性条件的等价性；将循环单调性概念成功推广至弱最优输运，表现为一阶最优性条件；在两类问题中，该性质均能刻画最优输运计划（在适当假设下）。

Conclusion: 本文从变分最优性条件的角度重新审视了循环单调性，为经典和弱最优输运问题提供了统一的最优性刻画框架，深化了对这一重要概念的理论理解。

Abstract: The celebrated c-cyclical monotonicity property is shown to boil down to the zeroth-order optimality condition for the optimal transport problem. More precisely, we show that optimality is equivalent to the non-negativity of the linear transport cost functional on the radial cone of admissible perturbations. We then utilise this point of view to extend the c-cyclical monotonicity property to the weak optimal transport problem, for which it corresponds to the first-order optimality condition, namely to the non-negativity of the linearisation of the weak transport cost functional near the optimiser. Altogether, this sheds new light on this monotonicity concept. For both classical and weak optimal transport, we show that this property characterises (under suitable assumptions) optimal transport plans. In the classical case, we recover known results of the literature but with revisited proofs.

</details>


### [97] [On the Limits of Biased Derivative Information for Nonconvex Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19553)
*Anant Shyam,Brian Bullins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在随机且带偏导的梯度oracle下，寻找非凸光滑目标的δ-稳定点的下界与上界。一阶情形下给出了匹配已有上界的紧下界；高阶情形下建立了依赖于偏差的下界，并提出了信赖域方法和高阶方差缩减方案，在高偏差场景下展现了高阶信息的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注无偏随机梯度场景，但实际应用中梯度oracle常存在偏差（如异步计算、压缩通信等），且高阶方法的理论界限尚不清晰。本文旨在刻画偏差对一阶和高阶算法寻找稳定点的影响，并探索高阶信息能否在偏差场景下突破无偏场景的限制。

Method: 1. 一阶情形：构造紧下界，匹配Ajalloeian和Stich (2020)的上界；2. 高阶情形：建立偏差依赖的下界；3. 设计信赖域方法匹配某些偏差范围的下界；4. 提出高阶方差缩减方案，在高偏差场景下改进Oracle复杂度。

Result: 一阶：找到O((ε+B²)^(1/2))-稳定点是紧的。高阶：找到O(ε+B_max)-稳定点的下界与偏差相关；信赖域方法在特定偏差范围内匹配下界；高阶方差缩减在高偏差场景下优于一阶方法。

Conclusion: 在存在梯度偏差的非凸优化中，高阶导数信息可以在高偏差场景下带来显著的Oracle复杂度改进，而在无偏随机场景中这种改进是不可能的。这揭示了偏差场景与无偏场景的本质区别，并提供了匹配的上下界理论。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding $δ$-stationary points for $δ> 0$, i.e., $x \in \mathbb{R}^d$ such that $||\nabla F(x)|| \le δ$, for smooth, non-convex objectives, where the derivative oracles are not only stochastic but also biased. In the first-order setting, we provide tight lower bounds for finding an $O((ε+ B^2)^{1/2})$-stationary point, for $ε> 0$ and where $B$ is a bound on the gradient bias, matching the upper bounds of Ajalloeian and Stich (2020). We then establish bias-dependent lower bounds for algorithms that use higher-order derivative information for finding $O(ε+ B_{\max})$-stationary points, where $B_{\max}$ is a bound on the maximum bias for all derivatives. To complement these lower bounds, we develop trust-region based methods that, for certain ranges of bias, provide guarantees that match the corresponding lower bounds. We further improve upon the oracle complexity in high bias settings through a higher-order variance reduction scheme, in particular demonstrating the benefits, in some cases, of using higher-order derivative information, whereas such improvements are known to be unattainable for stochastic unbiased settings.

</details>


### [98] [Mean-Field Control with a Common Hidden State under Decentralized Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19639)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ali D. Kara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有多个决策者、共享隐藏状态且通过相同信道接收分散观测的系统最优控制。当智能体数量趋于无穷时，问题简化为单智能体控制问题，其中智能体通过动作的条件律影响隐藏状态。本文证明了随机化控制动作对最优性的必要性，但混合策略非必需；同时证明了无限人口问题的最优对称策略对有限人口问题具有近最优性，收敛速度为$1/\sqrt{N}$，且随策略记忆长度指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多决策者系统中，因共享隐藏状态和分散观测导致的高维度复杂性，通过均场极限将问题降维为单智能体控制问题。

Method: 将无限智能体极限问题建模为确定性测度值控制问题，定义在策略空间上并建立动态规划递归。通过理论分析证明随机化控制动作的必要性及混合策略的非必要性，并建立有限人口与无限人口问题之间的收敛速率。

Result: 随机化控制动作是达到最优的必要条件，但混合策略（对策略本身的随机化）不必要；无限人口最优对称策略对有限人口具有$1/\sqrt{N}$的收敛速率，该速率随策略记忆长度指数增长。

Conclusion: 为具有共享隐藏状态和分散观测的多智能体最优控制问题提供了理论框架和收敛保证，揭示了随机化在动作层面而非策略层面的关键作用。

Abstract: We study optimal control of a system with multiple decision makers who share a common hidden state and receive fully decentralized observations through identical channels. The dynamics of the hidden state and the cost incurred by the agents depend on the agents' actions only through their empirical distribution. In the limit problem with infinitely many agents, the problem reduces to a single agent control problem where the agent affects the hidden state dynamics via the conditional law of the actions given the past values of the hidden state process. We formulate this problem as a deterministic measure valued control problem over the space of policies and provide a dynamic programming recursion.
  We first show that for the limiting problem randomization over the control actions is necessary for optimality. However, randomization over the selection of policies (i.e., mixture policies) is not required. We then show that the optimal symmetric policies designed for the infinite population problem are near optimal for the finite population problem.
  In particular, we establish convergence rates that decay with number of agents as $\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}$, and grow exponentially with the memory length used in the policy.

</details>


### [99] [Counterexample to a conjecture on the pairwise independent correlation gap using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19663)
*Arjun Ramachandra,Karthik Natarajan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文借助AI工具GPT5.5 Pro，构造了一个反例，反驳了Ramachandra与Natarajan在2025年提出的关于两两独立相关间隙的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳Ramachandra和Natarajan（2025）提出的关于两两独立相关间隙的猜想，推动该理论问题的进展。

Method: 利用AI工具GPT5.5 Pro辅助构造反例。

Result: 成功构造出反例，证否了原猜想。
