Abstract: Aided by the AI tool GPT5.5 Pro, we provide a counterexample to a conjecture made by Ramachandra and Natarajan (2025) [Pairwise independent correlation gap, Operations Research Letters, 107255, 6040].

</details>


### [100] [Learning Neural Maximal Lyapunov Functions on $\mathsf{SO}(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19669)
*Adeel Akhtar,Matthieu Barreau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于对数映射的神经Lyapunov框架，用于在特殊正交群SO(n)上学习最大Lyapunov函数，并通过Zubov型特征化刻画最大吸引域，同时推导了对数映射导数的显式可计算公式，通过两阶段算法平衡计算效率与精度，在低维非线性系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李群上动力系统的稳定性保证是一个基本挑战，因为经典Lyapunov方法在弯曲几何上无法直接迁移。

Method: 提出基于对数映射的神经Lyapunov架构，具有可证明的逼近能力；利用Zubov型特征化将学习问题公式化；关键贡献是推导了对数映射导数的显式、数值可计算的公式，并通过两阶段算法进行训练。

Result: 在低维非线性系统上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效学习SO(n)上的最大Lyapunov函数并刻画最大吸引域，为弯曲几何上的稳定性分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Establishing stability guarantees for dynamical systems on Lie groups is a fundamental challenge, as classical Lyapunov methods developed for Euclidean spaces do not directly transfer to curved geometries. In this paper, we propose a framework for learning maximal Lyapunov functions for systems evolving on the special orthogonal group $\mathsf{SO}(n)$. Theoretically, we introduce a neural Lyapunov architecture based on the logarithmic map with proven approximation capabilities, and we formulate the learning problem via a Zubov-type characterization of the maximal region of attraction. A key technical contribution is the derivation of explicit, numerically tractable formulas for the derivative of the logarithmic map, enabling training through a two-phase algorithm that balances computational efficiency and accuracy. Empirically, we validate the approach on a low-dimensional nonlinear system.

</details>


### [101] [Stochastic Representations of Stationary HJBI-Type Variational Inequalities with Bilateral Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19705)
*Sheng Huang,Qingmeng Wei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带有双边障碍的稳态HJBI型变分不等式，提出了两种互补的随机表示：一是通过扩展控制空间加入两个额外停时符号的无限时域两人零和随机微分博弈；二是通过两人零和混合控制-停止随机微分博弈，其中每个玩家同时选择连续控制和停止决策，支付由带有随机终端时间的BSDE定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为稳态HJBI型双边障碍变分不等式建立概率表示，弥补该领域在随机表示方面的理论空白。

Method: (1) 通过扩大控制空间引入两个额外停时符号，将障碍项纳入运行支付，利用无限时域随机递归微分博弈框架；(2) 引入依赖于对手控制过程的非预期停止策略，结合惩罚无限时域SDG及其值函数、动态规划论证和向后半群的稳定性估计。

Result: 两种表示得到的下值和上值函数均为对应HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解。

Conclusion: 混合控制-停止博弈的值函数与双边HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解一致，为这类变分不等式提供了完整的概率表示理论。

Abstract: In this paper, we study probabilistic representations for stationary HJBI-type variational inequalities with bilateral constraints. We provide two complementary stochastic representations.The first representation is obtained through an augmented infinite-horizon two-player zero-sum stochastic differential game (SDG). By enlarging the control spaces with two additional stopping symbols, the obstacle terms are incorporated into the running payoff. Using the framework of infinite-horizon stochastic recursive differential games, we show that the resulting lower and upper value functions are the unique bounded viscosity solutions of the corresponding HJBI variational inequalities. The second representation is given by a two-player zero-sum mixed control--stopping SDG. In this formulation, each player chooses both a continuous control and a stopping decision, and the payoff is defined by a BSDE with a random terminal time. To make the stopping component compatible with the Elliott--Kalton strategy framework, we introduce nonanticipative stopping strategies depending on the opponent's control process. The proof is based on penalized infinite-horizon SDGs coupled with their own value functions, together with dynamic programming arguments and stability estimates for backward semigroups. We prove that the value functions of the mixed control--stopping game coincide with the unique bounded viscosity solutions of the bilateral HJBI variational inequalities.

</details>


### [102] [Signature Methods for Optimal Market Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19772)
*Alberto Gennaro,Thibaut Mastrolia,Francesca Primavera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于签名（signature）的方法来解决均值-方差准则下的最优做市问题。通过签名线性化技术，将做市问题简化为对增强市场路径期望签名的伪线性优化，并开发了名为Sig-REINFORCE的签名算法来学习最优买卖报价。在两种场景下测试（泊松过程和自激霍克斯过程），并与PPO基线进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决均值-方差准则下最优做市问题的求解困难，利用签名方法实现问题的线性化转化，从而高效学习最优报价策略。

Method: 1) 采用签名线性化技术将市场做市问题转化为对增强市场路径期望签名的伪线性优化问题；2) 开发了名为Sig-REINFORCE的签名算法来学习最优买卖报价。

Result: 在泊松过程和自激霍克斯过程两种市场订单到达场景下进行了测试，并与近端策略优化（PPO）基线进行了对比。

Conclusion: 提出的签名方法能有效解决均值-方差准则下的最优做市问题。

Abstract: We propose a signature-based method to solve the optimal market-making problem under a mean-variance criterion. By exploiting signature linearization techniques, we reduce the market-making problem to a pseudo-linear optimization over the expected signature of an augmented market path, and we develop a signature algorithm named Sig-REINFORCE to learn the optimal bid and ask quotes. We test our method in two scenarios, in which market-order arrivals follow either a Poisson or a self-exciting Hawkes process, and we benchmark it against a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) baseline.

</details>


### [103] [Dynamic Core Allocation for Malleable Jobs with Unknown Speed-up Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19789)
*S. ~A. Bodas,J. ~L. Dorsman,M. Mandjes,L. Ravner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对多核计算系统中可塑作业的动态资源分配问题，提出了一种迭代学习与控制框架，在未知加速比参数下通过最大似然估计与MDP策略更新相结合，最小化长期平均作业数（即平均响应时间）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核系统中可塑作业的并行度可动态调整，但作业加速比函数参数未知，需要在不确知参数条件下学习最优核心分配策略以最小化平均响应时间。

Method: 构建迭代学习-控制框架：交替执行基于状态相关离去间隔时间的最大似然估计（证明强一致性）与求解MDP更新分配策略。类内核心均分，类间分配比例由当前参数估计下的MDP给出。

Result: 证明了固定分配策略下最大似然估计量的强一致性；提出了两种结合估计与动态规划策略更新的学习算法，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够在未知加速比参数条件下有效学习核心分配策略，最小化系统长期平均作业数。

Abstract: We study dynamic resource allocation in a multicore computing system with a fixed number of processing cores and a stream of {\it malleable} jobs. Each job may adjust its level of parallelism during execution, allowing adaptive redistribution of resources across concurrently active jobs. Jobs belong to one of two observable classes, each characterized by a distinct speed-up function with unknown parameters. The objective is to learn a core-allocation policy that minimizes the long-run mean number of jobs in the system, equivalently the mean response time in steady state.
  \noindent To address this uncertainty, we develop an iterative learning-and-control framework. The system alternates between estimating the unknown speed-up parameters from observed job completions and solving the associated Markov decision process (MDP) to update the allocation policy. Within each job class, cores are shared equally among active jobs; the fraction of capacity assigned to each class is obtained from the MDP formulation of \cite{berg2017}, evaluated at the current parameter estimates. We construct a maximum likelihood estimator based on state-dependent inter-departure times and prove its strong consistency under a fixed allocation policy. We further propose two learning algorithms that combine this estimation step with dynamic programming-based policy updates, and illustrate their through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [104] [Semiglobal Input-Delay Tolerance Algorithm for Distributed Nonconvex Optimization of Networked Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19871)
*Jing-Zhe Xu,Zhi-Wei Liu,Ming-Feng Ge,Yan-Wu Wang,Dinxin He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带有输入时滞和一致性约束的网络化非线性系统，提出了一种半全局输入时滞容忍（SIDT）分布式优化算法，在非严格凸条件下实现了输入时滞容忍的半全局收敛（IDTSC），并通过数值实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式优化算法大多假设理想通信或无时滞条件，而实际网络化非线性系统中普遍存在输入时滞，这会导致算法收敛性恶化甚至失效。本文旨在填补输入时滞与非线性动力学耦合下分布式优化理论空白，建立时滞容忍的收敛保证。

Method: 采用层次化设计框架，结合输入-状态稳定性（ISS）分析，提出半全局输入时滞容忍（SIDT）算法；通过引入Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件放宽对目标函数严格凸性的要求，将理论推广至非凸优化场景。

Result: 理论证明了所提SIDT算法在实际意义上实现了IDTSC，即在任意给定的紧致初始集上存在可容许的时滞上界，使得所有节点状态在满足一致性约束的条件下收敛到最优解。数值实验验证了算法在输入时滞存在下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为带输入时滞的网络化非线性系统提供了首个半全局时滞容忍的分布式优化理论框架，并通过放松凸性假设拓展了适用范围，为实际工程中的时滞鲁棒分布式优化提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of distributed optimization problems in networked nonlinear systems (NNSs) subject to input delays and consensus constraints. It introduces input-delay tolerant semiglobal convergence (IDTSC), meaning that for any prescribed compact initial set there exists an admissible delay bound under which the optimal solution is computed within consensus constraints and all node states converge to the solution. Building on a hierarchical design and input-to-state stability analysis, a new semiglobal input-delay tolerant (SIDT) algorithm is developed that practically achieves IDTSC for distributed optimization under the coupling between input delays and nonlinear dynamics. Further, by relaxing strict convexity requirements through the Polyak-Łojasiewicz condition, the SIDT algorithm broadens its applicability to nonconvex optimization. Finally, numerical experiments corroborate the theory on NNSs with input delays.

</details>


### [105] [A Regularized Nikaido-Isoda Function Approach to Multi-Leader-Follower Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20013)
*Atsushi Hori,Takayuki Okuno,Ellen H. Fukuda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多领导者-跟随者博弈（MLFG）提出了一种新的单层可微纳什均衡重构方法，利用正则化Nikaido-Isoda函数和惩罚参数，无需跟随者博弈的导数信息或凸性假设，适用于更广泛的MLFG类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLFG求解方法（如EPEC或代入响应函数）存在可扩展性差、需要高阶导数或依赖跟随者问题凸性等局限，难以处理更一般的MLFG问题。

Method: 利用正则化Nikaido-Isoda函数，将MLFG近似为带惩罚参数的单层可微纳什均衡问题，无需跟随者博弈的导数信息或凸性假设。

Result: 在全局子解析性条件下，建立了原始MLFG均衡与所提重构问题之间的数学关系。

Conclusion: 所提方法可处理更广泛的MLFG问题，且不依赖跟随者博弈的凸性或导数信息，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: A multi-leader--follower game (MLFG) is a hierarchical noncooperative game in which leaders compete at the upper level while taking into account the followers' best responses at the lower level. A typical approach to solving the MLFG reformulates it as an equilibrium problem with equilibrium constraints (EPECs) by replacing the lower-level game with its KKT conditions. Another approach, when each follower's response is unique, is to reformulate the MLFG as a Nash equilibrium problem by substituting these response functions into each leader's problem. However, both reformulations may lack scalability since higher-order derivatives may be required when solving the resulting problems.
  In this paper, we propose a new reformulation of the MLFG by exploiting a regularized Nikaido--Isoda function and approximating the MLFG by a single-level differentiable Nash equilibrium problem with a penalty parameter. The proposed reformulation neither requires derivative information on the followers' game nor assumes convexity of each follower's problem; hence, it can handle a broader class of MLFGs. Under global subanalyticity, we analyze the mathematical relationship between equilibria of the original MLFG and the proposed reformulation.

</details>


### [106] [Optimization with inequality constraints by the embedded gradient vector field method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20059)
*Petre Birtea,Ioan Casu,Dan Comanescu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入二次松弛变量，将带不等式约束的优化问题提升到黎曼几何框架中，利用嵌入梯度向量场方法求解。作者详细分析了约束流形的分层结构，推导出拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式，重新阐释了KKT条件，并给出了二阶最优性条件的几何刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不等式约束优化问题主要依赖拉格朗日乘子法和KKT条件进行分析，但缺乏直接的几何解释。作者希望通过引入二次松弛变量，将可行集提升为光滑子流形，从而利用黎曼几何的语言和嵌入梯度向量场方法，为约束优化问题提供一种纯几何的求解框架。

Method: 1) 引入二次松弛变量将不等式约束转化为等式约束，将可行集提升为扩展环境空间中的光滑子流形；2) 详细分析约束流形的分层结构，根据活跃约束进行自然划分；3) 利用嵌入梯度向量场形式，推导拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式；4) 在每个分层上计算限制Hessian矩阵获得二阶最优性条件；5) 识别拉格朗日乘子的完整符号条件作为互补松弛条件的几何对应。

Result: 得到了拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式，重新推导了经典KKT一阶必要条件；获得了每个分层上的二阶最优性条件；建立了拉格朗日乘子符号条件与互补松弛条件之间的几何对应关系；以双冰激凌锥问题为例，展示了几何结构如何决定局部极小值的性质与数量。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为不等式约束优化问题提供了一种全新的视角，将KKT条件和最优性条件自然地嵌入到黎曼几何语言中，为理解和求解此类问题提供了有力的几何工具。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for constrained optimization problems with inequality constraints through the introduction of quadratic slack variables. This formulation makes it possible to employ the language of Riemannian geometry and to solve the problem via the embedded gradient vector field method. We lift the feasible set to a smooth submanifold of an extended ambient space. The stratified structure of the resulting constraint manifold is analyzed in detail, yielding a natural partition according to which constraints are active. Using the embedded gradient vector field formalism, we derive explicit, determinantal formulas for the Lagrange multiplier functions directly from the geometry of the constraint manifold, recovering and re-framing the classical Karush-Kuhn-Tucker first-order necessary conditions without invoking the classical Lagrange multiplier method. Second-order optimality conditions are obtained by computing the restricted Hessian on each stratum, and a complete sign condition on the Lagrange multipliers is identified as the geometric counterpart of the classical complementary slackness condition. The theory is illustrated on the double ice-cream cone example, where the geometry of the problem determines the nature and number of local minima.

</details>


### [107] [Optimal Coarse Correlated Equilibria in Mean Field Games: Linear Programming and No-Regret Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20062)
*Luciano Campi,Federico Cannerozzi,Ioannis Tzouanas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间平均场博弈，提出了最优粗相关均衡的概念，建立了线性规划刻画，证明了存在性，并设计了无遗憾原始-对偶学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续时间平均场博弈中，已有均衡概念（如纳什均衡）计算复杂或难以优化社会目标，亟需一种既能保证激励兼容性又能优化外部性能指标的均衡选择框架。

Method: 1）提出均值场粗相关均衡的线性规划（LP）形式化；2）证明LP解与概率均衡的等价关系；3）基于外部遗憾约束的拉格朗日对偶形式，设计无遗憾原始-对偶学习算法，并给出显式收敛速率。

Result: 建立了最优粗相关均衡的LP存在性定理，给出了学习算法的显式收敛速率，并通过数值算例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 连续时间平均场博弈的最优粗相关均衡可通过LP高效求解，且所提学习算法能收敛到该均衡，为大规模多智能体系统提供了一种可计算的最优协调方案。

Abstract: We introduce optimal coarse correlated equilibria for continuous-time mean field games. A coarse correlated equilibrium is a randomized recommendation scheme from which no player can gain by ignoring the recommendation and switching to an alternative strategy. The problem is as follows: a moderator selects, among all mean-field coarse correlated equilibria, one that optimizes a prescribed performance criterion, which may differ from the representative player's objective. After formulating the problem, we develop a linear programming (LP) formulation, prove the existence of optimal LP coarse correlated equilibria, and relate the LP characterization to the original probabilistic setting. Building on this characterization, we design a no-regret primal-dual algorithm, based on an equivalent Lagrangian formulation of the external-regret constraint, for learning such equilibria. We provide explicit convergence rates for the learning algorithm, and numerical examples illustrate the method.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Averaging in John Ellipsoid Approximation: High-Accuracy Algorithms in the Leverage-Score Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20082)
*Xiaoyu Li,Junwei Yu,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了对称多面体John椭球的经典算法，将现代算法中混淆的三种成本（验证、识别、精度）分离，指出迭代复杂度中的ε⁻¹项仅来自验证成本。通过D-最优设计等价形式，证明均匀平均迭代的Frank-Wolfe间隙为Θ(1/T)，而使用热启动加速方法后，精度依赖仅为双对数级别：O(d² log log(1/ε))，主要瓶颈转移至识别成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示现有John椭球/杠杆分数算法中复杂度来源的结构，将验证、识别、精度三种成本分离，指出真正的计算瓶颈所在。

Method: 通过将原问题等价转化为D-最优设计形式，建立杠杆分数oracle与一阶oracle的对应关系，分别分析均匀平均迭代（验证成本）和最后迭代（热启动加速+阻尼牛顿）的收敛行为。

Result: 均匀平均迭代的Frank-Wolfe间隙为Θ(1/T)，验证成本导致ε⁻¹依赖；而热启动加速方法仅需C(A)+O(√κ log(1/ε))次查询，阻尼牛顿阶段仅需O(d² log log(1/ε))次查询，精度依赖降至双对数级别。

Conclusion: 精度不是计算瓶颈；主要开放问题是最优面的识别成本，即到达最优面的无条件界和下界。

Abstract: The John ellipsoid of a symmetric polytope $P=\{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^d:\|\mathbf{A}\mathbf{x}\|_\infty\le1\}$, $\mathbf{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, is computed by a long line of leverage-score algorithms, from Cohen, Cousins, Lee and Yang (COLT 2019) to its successors [WY24, CLS+25], all reaching a $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation in $Θ(\varepsilon^{-1}\log(n/d))$ iterations. We separate this complexity into three costs the modern line conflates (certification, identification, and accuracy) and locate the historical $\varepsilon^{-1}$ in the first alone. In the equivalent D-optimal-design form $\min_{\mathbf{p}\inΔ_n}-\log\det(\sum_i p_i\mathbf{a}_i\mathbf{a}_i^\top)$, the leverage-score oracle is exactly the first-order oracle and the $(1+\varepsilon)$-John guarantee the Frank-Wolfe gap $g(\mathbf{p})\le\varepsilon d$; through this dictionary the costs come apart. The $\varepsilon^{-1}$ is a certification artifact: the uniform average of the iterates, the certificate used throughout the line, has gap exactly $Θ(1/T)$, however cheap each iteration is made. Pointed instead at the last iterate the same oracle is fast: a warm-started accelerated method reaches the guarantee in $C(\mathbf{A})+O(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries after an $\varepsilon$-independent setup $C(\mathbf{A})$, and once the optimal face is identified the facial problem is an unconstrained self-concordant minimization whose Hessian the oracle recovers exactly, so damped Newton needs only $O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ steps, for a total of $C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries. The accuracy dependence is thus doubly logarithmic after an $\varepsilon$-independent, condition-dependent setup; the open problem is the remaining identification cost (a condition-free bound on reaching the optimal face) and lower bounds. Accuracy is not the obstruction.

</details>


### [109] [Optimizing Agricultural Drone Operations: From Launch and Recovery Siting to Tiered Routing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20239)
*Ethan Kolby,Josh Noble,Max Z. Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对农业无人机喷洒作业，提出了一种结合设施选址启发式算法与分层路由分解的操作框架，在保障服务面积仅下降4%的前提下，将规划时间从97秒以上降至1.2秒以内，实现了分钟级规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业无人机作业面临利润率低、规划问题规模大导致传统优化工具运行时间指数增长的问题，亟需实用的启发式方法支持日常运营。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：设施选址阶段对比MIP基线模型与p-中值启发式算法；路径规划阶段采用分层问题分解方法，将目标区域划分为6至8个空间簇进行求解。

Result: p-中值启发式算法将运行时间从97秒以上降至1.2秒以内（降低三个数量级），服务面积仅下降4%；分层路由分解使计算时间降低一个数量级，服务面积几乎没有下降。

Conclusion: 该框架在商用硬件上实现了分钟级规划，具备实际运营可行性；未来工作将引入天气建模、设施选址与路由的联合优化，并在多种田间几何形状下进行验证。

Abstract: Drones are increasingly used in agriculture, where tight margins demand efficient planning. Current optimization tools suffer from exponential runtimes as problem sizes grow, necessitating practical heuristics for daily operations. This paper presents an operational framework and benchmarking analysis for drone spraying operations. We evaluate the trade-offs between facility siting methods and tiered routing parameters. For facility siting, comparing a Mixed-Integer Program (MIP) baseline against a $p$-Median heuristic shows that the heuristic reduces runtime by three orders of magnitude, from over 97 seconds to under 1.2 seconds, with only a 4\% reduction in serviced field area. For route planning, a tiered problem decomposition approach partitioning the target area into 6 to 8 spatial clusters reduces computation time by an order of magnitude with minimal degradation in serviced area. This framework achieves minute-scale planning on commodity hardware, demonstrating operational relevance. Future research will incorporate weather modeling, integrated optimization of facility location and routing, and validation across diverse field geometries.

</details>


### [110] [Diagonal Hessian Approximation Based on Conjugacy Condition for Noisy Derivative-Free Optimization Problems in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20304)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Saman Babaie--Kafaki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对大规模含噪无导数优化问题，提出了一种使用对角近似替代全仿射尺度矩阵的进化策略方法，通过保守对角更新和共轭型条件，在噪声环境下比MAES类方法更高效稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模含噪无导数优化中，MAES类方法在噪声较大时排序选择不可靠，导致重组步骤和仿射/矩阵适应机制效能下降，尤其在高维场景下效率严重降低。

Method: 用共轭型条件构造的对角近似替代全仿射尺度矩阵；利用连续归一化重组位移进行保守对角更新，避免依赖梯度、次梯度、插值模型及含噪排序的协方差信息。

Result: 计算成本低于全矩阵适应和有限内存仿射尺度变体；在含噪基准问题上的数值实验表明，该方法与MAES类基线相比具有竞争力，在噪声大、排序选择不可靠时往往更高效。

Conclusion: 提出的对角更新策略在保留无导数进化框架的同时，有效抑制了噪声对选择信息的干扰，在噪声水平高的场景下提供稳定的缩放机制，是MAES类方法的有效替代方案。

Abstract: We consider large-scale noisy derivative-free optimization (DFO) problems in which only function values are available and gradient or subgradient information cannot be reliably estimated. Matrix-adaptation evolution strategies (MAES) and their limited-memory variants are among the most robust DFO methods under noise; however, their performance may deteriorate when the noise level is large. In such regimes, sorting and selection may misidentify informative sampled points, making the recombination step less reliable and weakening the scaling information used by affine or matrix-adaptation mechanisms. This can substantially reduce the efficiency of MAES-type methods, especially in high-dimensional settings.
  To address this limitation, we propose a DFO method that replaces the full affine-scaling matrix with a diagonal approximation constructed from conjugacy-type conditions. The proposed mechanism does not attempt to estimate gradients, subgradients, or interpolation models, nor does it learn dense covariance information from noisy rankings. Instead, it uses consecutive normalized recombination displacements in a conservative diagonal update, thereby limiting the influence of unreliable selection information while preserving the derivative-free structure of the underlying evolutionary framework. As a result, the method is computationally cheaper than full matrix-adaptation schemes and limited-memory affine-scaling variants, while providing a stable scaling mechanism in noisy environments. Numerical experiments on noisy benchmark problems show that the proposed method is competitive with, and often more efficient than, MAES-type baselines, particularly when the noise level is large and ranking-based selection becomes unreliable.

</details>


### [111] [Robust $Q$-learning for mean-field control under Wasserstein uncertainty in common noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20356)
*Mathieu Laurière,Ariel Neufeld,Kyunghyun Park*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Wasserstein不确定性下离散时间均场控制问题的鲁棒Q学习算法，该算法结合量化投影方案与Wasserstein对偶重构，证明了同步和异步学习方案的收敛性与有限时间迭代界，并通过系统风险和流行病模型实验验证了算法的鲁棒性与性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有均场控制方法在处理公共噪声律的模型不确定性时缺乏鲁棒性，特别是当真实分布偏离假设模型时性能显著下降。本文旨在设计一种能够对抗Wasserstein不确定性（即公共噪声分布在一个Wasserstein球内变化）的鲁棒强化学习算法。

Method: 提出一种鲁棒Q学习算法，核心创新包括：(1) 量化投影方案将连续状态空间离散化；(2) 利用Wasserstein对偶重构将鲁棒优化问题转化为可计算形式；(3) 同步和异步两种学习方案，并给出有限时间迭代收敛界分析。

Result: 理论结果：同步和异步学习方案均收敛，并给出了有限时间迭代界。实验验证：在系统风险和流行病模型上，异步实现与理想化Bellman迭代进行了比较，展示了公共噪声误设下的鲁棒性-性能权衡，并报告了异步Q学习算法的实际收敛行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒Q学习算法能有效处理均场控制中公共噪声律的Wasserstein不确定性，兼具理论收敛保证和实际计算可行性，为不确定环境下的多智能体决策提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: In this article, we present a robust $Q$-learning algorithm for discrete-time mean-field control problems under Wasserstein uncertainty in the common noise law. The algorithm combines a quantization-and-projection scheme with a Wasserstein dual reformulation on the common-noise space. We establish its convergence together with finite-time iteration bounds for both synchronous and asynchronous learning schemes. Numerical experiments on systemic risk and epidemic models compare the asynchronous implementation with an idealized Bellman iteration, illustrate the robustness-performance tradeoff under common-noise misspecification, and report the observed convergence behavior of the asynchronous $Q$-learning algorithm.

</details>


### [112] [A Single-Loop Minorized Dual Decomposition Method for Nonsmooth Multi-Stage Stochastic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20383)
*Dan Luo,Hailin Sun,Lei Yang,Yang You*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑复合目标的多阶段随机规划问题，提出了一种单循环极小化对偶分解方法，通过构造极小化问题及其Wolfe对偶，并采用对称高斯-赛德尔交替方向乘子法单步求解对偶问题，实现了阶段与场景可分解的并行计算框架，证明了全局收敛性，数值实验验证了其扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机规划问题具有内在的阶段-场景结构，现有方法在保持这种可分解结构的同时实现高效求解存在挑战，尤其是对于非光滑复合目标函数的情形。

Method: 提出单循环极小化对偶分解方法：每轮迭代先构造极小化问题及其受限Wolfe对偶，然后在该对偶问题上执行一步对称高斯-赛德尔非精确交替方向乘子法以产生下一轮迭代点。

Result: 建立了三阶段情形的全局收敛性，并推广到一般多阶段情形的全局收敛定理；数值实验展示了方法的计算可行性和在阶段与场景维度上的良好缩放行为。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效保持了MSP问题的阶段-场景可分解结构，适合并行实现，且在理论收敛性和计算效率上均具有优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi-stage stochastic programming (MSP) problems with nonsmooth composite objectives. Tailored to their intrinsic stage-wise and scenario-wise structure, we develop a single-loop minorized dual decomposition method, in which each iteration constructs a minorized problem and its restricted Wolfe dual, and then performs \textit{one iteration} of the symmetric Gauss--Seidel based inexact alternating direction method of multipliers on the resulting dual problem to generate the next iterate. A key feature of the proposed optimization framework is that the resulting updates preserve the stage-wise and scenario-wise decomposable structure of the MSP problem and are suitable for parallel implementation. We establish global convergence of the generated iterates for the three-stage case and further establish the corresponding global convergence theorem for the general multi-stage setting. Numerical experiments illustrate the computational viability of the proposed framework and its favorable scaling behavior with respect to the stage-wise and scenario-wise structure.

</details>


### [113] [High-Probability Last-Iterate Guarantees for Two-Point Gaussian Zeroth-Order Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20446)
*Haishan Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对光滑强凸随机优化问题，研究了同样本两点高斯零阶随机梯度方法，在条件指数矩假设下，以概率至少1-δ建立了O(d/T)的最后一迭代高概率收敛界，且置信度依赖为对数级而非多项式级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶随机优化方法的高概率分析通常依赖马氏不等式将期望界转换为概率界，或需要对噪声进行截断处理，缺乏直接的高概率最后一迭代收敛保证。

Method: 采用同样本两点高斯零阶随机梯度方法，每次迭代抽取高斯方向并用相同随机样本在对称扰动处评估目标函数，进行范数归一化的随机逼近步；证明利用了高斯角度的均匀加权扫描与角度扩展的乘积鞅边界来控制未展开随机递归中的带符号后缀乘积项。

Result: 在d≥16log(6T/δ)条件下，f(x_T)-f(x*)=Õ(d/T)以至少1-δ的概率成立，置信度依赖为对数级。

Conclusion: 首次为同样本高斯零阶递归在条件次高斯噪声下建立了直接的高概率最后一迭代收敛结果。

Abstract: We establish a direct high-probability last-iterate guarantee for the standard same-sample two-point Gaussian zeroth-order stochastic-gradient method applied to smooth, strongly convex stochastic optimization. At each iteration, the method draws a fresh Gaussian direction, evaluates the objective at two symmetric perturbations using the same stochastic sample, and takes a norm-normalized stochastic-approximation step. Assuming unbiased stochastic gradients and a conditional exponential-moment bound on the squared norm of the stochastic-gradient noise, we prove that, whenever \(d\ge16\log(6T/δ)\), \[ f(\bx_T)-f(\bx^*) =
\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left(\frac{d}{T}\right) \] with probability at least \(1-δ\), up to fixed problem parameters and logarithmic factors. The confidence dependence is therefore logarithmic rather than polynomial in \(1/δ\). The analysis is direct: it neither invokes Markov's inequality to convert an expectation bound nor truncates the noise. We are not aware of a prior direct high-probability last-iterate result at this zeroth-order scale for the same-sample Gaussian recursion under conditional sub-Gaussian stochastic-gradient noise. The proof combines a uniform weighted scan for Gaussian angles with an angle-enlarged product-martingale boundary that controls the signed suffix-product term arising from the unrolled stochastic recursion.

</details>


### [114] [CLUSTER: Derivative-free optimization of smooth functions with parameter-change costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20498)
*Serena Landers,Sahil Pontula,Shiekh Zia Uddin,Sachin Vaidya,Marin Soljačić,Steven G. Johnson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出CLUSTER算法，针对参数（或参数簇）调整有成本代价的局部无导数优化问题，在Powell和Conn的二次插值优化算法基础上改进，在测试问题（包括光学实验）上性能提升约50%，并大幅优于贝叶斯优化和Nelder-Mead算法，同时给出了收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人控制实验等场景中，调整每个参数（或参数簇）需要单独的运动成本，现有无导数优化算法未考虑这种成本结构，导致实际效率低下。

Method: 基于Powell和Conn的二次插值优化算法，引入坐标层面的更新策略来评估和细化信任域步长，使算法在每次迭代中优先选择成本更低的参数调整方案。

Result: 在多种测试问题（包括光学实验室实验）上性能提升约50%，且大幅优于贝叶斯优化和Nelder-Mead等常见实验室优化算法。

Conclusion: CLUSTER算法能够有效处理参数调整有成本的优化问题，在保持理论收敛保证的同时显著提升实际性能，适用于机器人控制实验等场景。

Abstract: We introduce the CLUSTER algorithm (\textbf{c}oordinate-\textbf{l}evel \textbf{u}pdate \textbf{s}trategy for \textbf{t}rust-region step \textbf{e}valuation \textbf{r}efinement) for local derivative-free optimization problems where there is a cost to changing each parameter (or clusters of parameters). For example, this type of cost model is appropriate for optimizing robot-controlled laboratory experiments, in which a robot may incur a separate motion for each parameter cluster to be adjusted. We build off of a class of quadratic-interpolation optimization algorithms by Powell and Conn that are known to perform well for twice-differentiable objectives (e.g. low-noise experiments), and show that the CLUSTER variants improve performance on a variety of test problems (including an optics laboratory experiment) by around 50$\%$, and greatly outperform common competing algorithms for laboratory optimization (Bayesian optimization and Nelder--Mead). We also adapt the convergence proof of the Conn algorithm to obtain a similar convergence guarantee for CLUSTER-Conn.

</details>


### [115] [On Second-Order Methods for Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20534)
*Jiawen Bi,Jiaxiang Li,Mingyi Hong,Shuzhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对双层优化中寻找二阶稳定点（SOSP）的问题，提出了首个确定性的单循环三次正则牛顿算法，并在非凸上层-强凸下层（NCSC）设定下实现了最优的$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$总复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化中寻找二阶稳定点的理论和实践仍未解决。尽管三次正则牛顿法（CRN）在单层优化中能实现最优的SOSP率，但在双层优化中应用二阶方法时，如何控制超梯度和超Hessian计算的精度问题尚不清楚。

Method: 首先设计了双循环CRN基线（达到最优外层率但需要多次下层求解）；然后提出单循环三次正则牛顿算法，将一步下层梯度更新与一步超梯度牛顿更新相结合。

Result: 证明了单循环算法具有$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$的确定性的总预言复杂度，这是最优的。同时指出某些直观简单的修改会导致收敛性失效。

Conclusion: 这是首个在未约束NCSC双层优化设定下，实现$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$最优收敛率的确定性单循环方法。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is an indispensable modeling tool for modern machine learning and engineering design. However, the theory and practice for finding second order stationary points in the context of bilevel optimization still remain largely unsettled. Even for bilevel optimization with strongly convex lower-level problem, the hyperfunction it induces is in general nonconvex. Although the Cubic Regularized Newton methods (CRN) famously achieve the optimal $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ SOSP (second-order stationary point) rate in single-level optimization, it is unclear how to control the accuracy of the hypergradient and hyper-Hessian computations in the context of applying the second-order methods to bilevel problems in order for the overall process to be efficient. In this paper, we set out to answer this question. In particular, we first formulate a double loop CRN baseline that achieves the optimal outer rate but requires repeated lower level solves. Next, we propose a single loop cubic regularized Newton algorithm that combines one lower-level gradient step with one Newton step for the hypergradient, and prove an overall deterministic $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ total oracle complexity, which is optimal. In addition, we illustrate that some intuitively simple modifications of our method may fail to hold up the convergence result. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first deterministic single loop method for unconstrained NCSC (non-convex upper-level and strongly convex lower-level) bilevel optimization setting that achieves the $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ optimal convergence rate for finding an $\varepsilon$-SOSP of the hyperfunction.

</details>

### [116] [Neural Architectures as Functional Priors in Physics-Informed Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19368)
*Sonia Rubio Herranz,Fernando Carlos López Hernández,Antonio López Montes*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经架构在常微分方程控制问题中作为隐式功能先验的作用，发现不同架构（MLP与傅里叶基KAN类架构）即使在相同问题设置下也会产生定性不同的控制策略，傅里叶架构倾向于产生更丰富的振荡控制，而平滑低频架构则产生更规则、节能的控制，揭示了功能特化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经架构在控制问题中作为隐式功能先验的角色，理解架构选择对受控动力学系统行为的影响，而非关注高度复杂的问题。

Method: 以线性RLC电路和非线性Duffing动力系统为测试平台，通过经典最优控制公式和PINN方法，比较MLP与傅里叶基KAN类架构在不同组合下的控制效果。

Result: 不同架构在相同方程、损失函数、初始/目标状态、训练参数和物理约束下，产生频谱结构、平滑度、能量分布和相空间行为显著不同的控制策略。

Conclusion: 观察到功能特化现象：傅里叶基架构产生更丰富振荡轨迹，低频偏置架构产生更规则节能的控制，表明不同神经架构可能更适合处理控制问题的不同功能组件。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the role of neural architectures as implicit functional priors in control problems governed by ordinary differential equations. Rather than focusing on highly complex problems, our objective is to investigate architecture-dependent effects in controlled dynamical systems within the simplest physically interpretable settings possible. In particular, we study a controlled linear RLC electrical circuit and a nonlinear Duffing-type dynamical system. Both systems are analyzed first through classical optimal-control formulations and later through PINN-based approaches. We compare different combinations of multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and Fourier-based KAN-like architectures, and analyze their influence on the resulting controls. The numerical experiments suggest that different architectural choices systematically generate qualitatively distinct controls, even under identical governing equations, loss functionals, initial and target states, training parameters and physical constraints. Significant differences appear in the spectral structure, smoothness, energy distribution, and phase-space behavior of the learned solutions. A central observation of this work is the emergence of a functional specialization phenomenon when the neural architectures are allowed sufficient freedom to shape the structure of the learned controls. More specifically, in the systems considered here, Fourier-based architectures tend to produce trajectories with richer oscillatory content, whereas smoother low-frequency-biased architectures tend to generate more regular and energetically efficient controls. This suggests that different functional components of the control problem may be handled more efficiently by different neural architectures, leading to an implicit specialization between state representation and control generation.

</details>


### [117] [Moreau-Yosida-based Kohn-Sham Inversion for Periodic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19471)
*Vebjørn H. Bakkestuen,Michael F. Herbst,Vegard Falmår,Markus Penz,Andre Laestadius*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在Moreau-Yosida正则化密度泛函理论框架下，研究了周期系统的密度-势能反演问题，通过正则化途径恢复Kohn-Sham交换相关势，并利用近端映射实现数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决周期性系统中从密度恢复Kohn-Sham交换相关势的反演问题，为无轨道密度泛函理论的发展提供理论支撑。

Method: 在周期齐次Sobolev空间中建立理论框架，证明非相互作用动能泛函在该拓扑下的下半连续性，利用Moreau-Yosida正则化和近端映射实现反演算法。

Result: 数值实验验证了该方法在Kohn-Sham方程和Gross-Pitaevskii方程中的有效性和性能表现。

Conclusion: Moreau-Yosida正则化结合近端映射为周期系统的密度-势能反演提供了一套严谨的理论和高效的数值方案。

Abstract: Density-potential inversion for periodic systems within Moreau-Yosida-regularised density-functional theory is investigated, both theoretically and numerically. We develop the framework in a periodic homogeneous Sobolev space and use it to recover the exchange-correlation potential of Kohn-Sham theory through a limiting procedure. A key analytical ingredient is the proof of lower semicontinuity of the non-interacting kinetic-energy functional in the chosen topology. The proximal mapping, together with its algorithmic evaluation, plays a central role in the resulting inversion scheme. Numerical experiments illustrate the performance and properties of the method for both the Kohn-Sham and Gross-Pitaevskii equations.

</details>


### [118] [Higher Accuracy Modular Data Assimilation for the Navier-Stokes Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19508)
*Troy Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将二阶向后差分格式(BDF2)与模块化两步微推数据同化相结合的方法，通过显式分析步骤实现隐式稳定性，降低了计算复杂度，并通过数值实验验证了其在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种准确高效的数据同化方法，在保持与标准全耦合数据同化相当精度的前提下，大幅降低计算复杂度和成本。

Method: 将BDF2时间离散化与模块化两步微推数据同化相结合，预测步使用BDF2格式求解Navier-Stokes方程，分析步通过显式公式实现隐式稳定性。理论方面给出了稳定性和误差估计的证明，数值方面通过实验评估算法性能。

Result: 数值实验表明，模块化数据同化方法在与标准全耦合方法保持相当精度的同时，显著降低了计算复杂度和成本。

Conclusion: 模块化数据同化是一种在保持精度前提下有效降低计算复杂度的方案，BDF2格式与其结合具备良好的稳定性和准确性。

Abstract: This paper develops an accurate and effective combination of second order backward differentiation time discretization (BDF2) with modular, 2-step nudging-based data assimilation \begin{align} \text{Forecast step: } \quad &\frac{3\widetilde{v}^{n+2}-4v^{n+1}+v^n}{2Δt}+\widetilde{v}^{n+2} \cdot \nabla \widetilde{v}^{n+2} - νΔ\widetilde{v}^{n+2} + \nabla q^{n+2}=f(x) \notag \\ &\nabla \cdot \widetilde{v}^{n+2} = 0 \notag \\ \text{Analysis step: } \quad &\frac{3v^{n+2}-3\widetilde{v}^{n+2}}{2Δt}-χI_H(u(t^{n+2})-v^{n+2})=0. \notag \end{align} If $I_H=I_H^2$, the analysis step can be made explicit, taking the form \begin{align} v^{n+2}=\widetilde{v}^{n+2}+\frac{2Δtχ}{3+2Δtχ}I_H(u^{n+2}-\widetilde{v}^{n+2}). \notag \end{align} This implies the analysis step has the stability property of an implicit step and lower complexity than an explicit analysis step. Stability and error estimates for the BDF2 scheme are presented along with their proofs. Numerical experiments are conducted to assess the performance of BDF2 modular assimilation algorithm. The results of the experiments support the conclusion that modular data assimilation has comparable accuracy to standard, fully coupled data assimilation while greatly reducing computational complexity and cost.

</details>


### [119] [Bregman-projected mirror methods for regularized stationary mean-field games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19611)
*Hussain Al Abdulaziz,Yuri Ashrafyan,Yeva Gevorgyan,Diogo Gomes*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有可分Hamiltonian和超二次增长的动力学的平稳平均场博弈系统，提出了基于Bregman投影的镜像迭代方法，通过低阶正则化将问题转化为变分不等式，证明了约束迭代的强收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理具有混合Lebesgue-Sobolev指数设定的平稳平均场博弈系统时，缺乏适配其自然Banach空间几何的迭代算法。本文旨在弥补这一空白，为低阶正则化的MFG系统设计并分析具有Bregman几何匹配的镜像迭代方法。

Method: 将低阶\barγ-Laplacian正则化的平稳MFG系统表述为L^{\barβ}(\mathbb T^d)\times W^{1,\barγ}(\mathbb T^d)上的变分不等式，引入与混合Lebesgue-Sobolev指数相匹配的Bregman几何，设计约束两步镜像方法（含冻结算子评估），推导一步Bregman不等式并利用步长可和性条件证明强收敛性。

Result: 对于每个固定正则化参数ε>0，约束迭代在自然可和性条件下强收敛到正则化变分不等式的唯一解；一维和二维数值实验在网格细化下展示了残差衰减，两步实现在离散化中表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提出的Bregman投影镜像迭代方法为低阶正则化MFG系统提供了理论保证的收敛算法，数值结果验证了其实际可行性，且两步实现具有改进的计算性能。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a Bregman-projected mirror iteration for low-order regularizations of stationary mean-field game (MFG) systems in their natural Banach space setting. For separable Hamiltonians of the form \(H(x,p,m)=H_0(x,p)-g(m)\), with quadratic or super-quadratic Hamiltonian growth and linear or super-linear density couplings, we formulate a low-order \(\barγ\)-Laplacian regularization of the stationary MFG system as a variational inequality on \(L^{\barβ}(\mathbb T^d)\times W^{1,\barγ}(\mathbb T^d)\). To approximate solutions of this regularized variational inequality, we introduce a Bregman geometry matched to the mixed Lebesgue--Sobolev exponents of the problem and analyze a constrained two-step mirror method with frozen operator evaluation. For the exact constrained iteration and each fixed regularization parameter \(\epsi>0\), we derive a one-step Bregman inequality and use it to prove that the constrained iteration converges strongly to the unique solution of the regularized variational inequality under natural summability conditions on the step sizes. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional models, validated against exact test solutions, illustrate residual decay under mesh refinement and suggest improved practical performance of the two-step implementation in the tested discretizations.

</details>


### [120] [Second order explicit splitting scheme for fluid-poroelastic structure interaction problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19811)
*Yifan Wang,Jeonghun Lee,Suncica Canic*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对固定域上的时变Stokes-Biot问题，开发并分析了一种全离散、二阶精度、显式分裂格式。该方法结合BDF2时间步进和二阶Adams-Bashforth界面数据外推，通过Robin重构实现Stokes和Biot子问题的独立并行求解。理论贡献在于严格的稳定性和误差分析，在抛物型CFL条件下证明了稳定性界，并通过投影框架推导了先验误差估计，数值实验验证了二阶时间收敛性和最优阶空间收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体-多孔弹性结构相互作用的显式分区方法因Stokes-Biot界面耦合条件的显式处理可能破坏稳定性，现有高效且可证明精确的分区方法仍然具有挑战性，因此需要开发一种既稳定又具有二阶精度的显式分裂格式。

Method: 采用BDF2时间离散与二阶Adams-Bashforth界面外推相结合，通过Robin重构实现界面条件解耦；利用BDF2能量恒等式、外推界面项的尖刻分解和离散迹估计证明稳定性；通过Fortin投影（流体变量）和Ritz型投影（多孔弹性变量）的投影框架推导误差估计。

Result: 在抛物型CFL条件下证明了封闭稳定性界；误差分析表明流体速度、结构速度、孔隙压力和弹性位移的总误差在整体能量范数下由网格尺寸的k次幂（k=1到3）与时间步长的平方之和的常数倍控制；数值实验确认了二阶时间收敛和最优阶空间收敛。

Conclusion: 所提出的显式分裂格式在保证稳定性的前提下实现了二阶时间精度和最优空间精度，且可扩展至移动域Navier-Stokes流体问题，具有超越固定域Stokes-Biot设置的适用性。

Abstract: Efficient and provably accurate partitioned methods for fluid-poroelastic structure interaction remain challenging because explicit treatment of the Stokes-Biot interface coupling condition can compromise stability. In this work, we develop and analyze a fully discrete, second-order, explicit splitting scheme for the time-dependent Stokes-Biot problem on fixed domains. The method combines BDF2 time stepping with second-order Adams-Bashforth extrapolation of interface data through a Robin reformulation, yielding a partitioned algorithm in which the Stokes and Biot subproblems are solved independently and in parallel at each time step.
  The main analytical contribution is a rigorous stability and error analysis for this second-order explicit coupling strategy. Using BDF2 energy identities, a sharp decomposition of the extrapolated interface terms, and discrete trace estimates, we prove a closed stability bound under a parabolic CFL condition. We then derive an a priori error estimate through a projection-based framework using a Fortin projection for the fluid variables and Ritz-type projections for the poroelastic variables. The analysis identifies consistency defects from BDF2 time discretization, Adams-Bashforth interface extrapolation, and the projected kinematic relation. It shows that the total errors in fluid velocity, structure velocity, pore pressure, and elastic displacement are bounded by C times the sum of the kth power of the mesh size and the square of the time step, for k from 1 to 3, in bulk energy norms.
  Numerical experiments with manufactured solutions confirm second-order temporal convergence and optimal-order spatial convergence. We also include a moving-domain example with Navier-Stokes fluid flow, demonstrating applicability beyond the fixed-domain Stokes-Biot setting analyzed.

</details>


### [121] [On a class of modified Cayley--Magnus methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19614)
*Sergio Blanes,Fernando Casas,Arieh Iserles*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一类新的数值积分器，用于求解系数矩阵稀疏且在二次矩阵李群中演化的非自治线性常微分方程。该方法避免计算矩阵指数，转而求解稀疏线性系统，适用于无界算子问题，并构建了四阶和六阶优化格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有李群积分器需要计算矩阵指数作用在向量上，计算成本高，且对无界算子问题不适用，因此需要更高效、更稳定的数值方法。

Method: 提出一类不依赖矩阵指数计算的新型李群积分器，通过求解一系列稀疏系数矩阵的线性系统来实现时间积分，并构建了四阶和六阶的优化格式。

Result: 在代表性数值算例中，所提出的四阶和六阶优化格式相比现有李群积分器展现出明显的性能优势。

Conclusion: 该类新积分器避免了矩阵指数计算，适合稀疏和无界算子问题，且高阶格式在数值表现上优于传统李群积分器。

Abstract: We introduce a new class of numerical integrators for the time integration of non-autonomous linear ordinary differential equations whose coefficient matrix is sparse and evolves within a quadratic matrix Lie group. In contrast to standard Lie group integrators, the proposed methods avoid the evaluation of matrix exponentials acting on vectors and instead rely on solving a sequence of linear systems with sparse coefficient matrices. Moreover, they are well suited for problems arising from unbounded operators, as they inherently produce bounded solutions. We construct optimised schemes of orders four and six and assess their performance on a representative numerical example, demonstrating clear advantages over existing Lie-group integrators.

</details>


### [122] [Explicit Fourier Integrator for the Periodic dNLS via Gauge Transformation: Low-Regularity Estimates in Discrete Bourgain Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19648)
*Lun Ji,Hang Li,Alexander Ostermann,Gangfan Zhong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对一维环面上的导数非线性薛定谔方程（DNLS），研究了一种滤波显式Fourier积分格式。通过周期性规范变换和频率截断，结合滤波指数欧拉离散化，并发展离散Bourgain空间框架处理周期设置下导数三次非线性的困难。对于初值 $u_0 \in H^s(\mathbb{T})$（$1/2 < s \le 5/2$），证明数值误差在 $H^{1/2}(\mathbb{T})$ 中为 $\mathcal{O}(τ^{s/2-1/4})$，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导数非线性薛定谔方程是等离子体物理和非线性光学中描述非线性色散波传播的基础模型。在周期域上求解该方程时，导数三次非线性项带来了局部光滑性缺失和共振相互作用增强的困难，需要发展有效的数值方法和理论分析框架。

Method: 采用周期性规范变换将原方程转化为更易处理的形式；引入频率截断模型；使用滤波指数欧拉离散化进行时间积分；针对周期域导数非线性的困难，建立了适应规范变换后方程的离散Bourgain空间框架，用于严格分析数值误差。

Result: 对于光滑性参数 $1/2 < s \le 5/2$ 的初值，证明了数值格式在 $H^{1/2}(\mathbb{T})$ 范数下的误差阶为 $\mathcal{O}(τ^{s/2-1/4})$。数值实验验证了该收敛阶，并展示了滤波格式对粗糙解的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为周期DNLS方程提供了一种收敛性有理论保证的滤波显式数值格式，特别是在解光滑性较低（$s$ 接近 $1/2$）的情况下仍能有效工作。所发展的离散Bourgain空间分析方法可推广至其他含导数非线性项的周期色散方程。

Abstract: The derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation is a fundamental model for the propagation of nonlinear dispersive waves in, for example, plasma physics and nonlinear optics. In this work, we consider this model on the one-dimensional torus and study a filtered explicit Fourier integrator for the corresponding periodic problem. After applying a periodic gauge transformation, we consider a frequency-truncated model and its filtered exponential-Euler discretization. The main difficulty comes from the derivative cubic nonlinearity in the periodic setting, since local smoothing is unavailable and resonant interactions are stronger than in the non-periodic case. To address this issue, we develop a discrete Bourgain-space framework adapted to the gauge-transformed equation. For initial data $u_0 \in H^s(\mathbb{T})$ with $1/2 < s \le 5/2$, we prove that the numerical error is of order $\mathcal{O}(τ^{s/2-1/4})$ in $H^{1/2}(\mathbb{T})$, where $τ$ denotes the employed time step size. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence behavior and demonstrate the effectiveness of the filtered scheme for rough solutions.

</details>


### [123] [A Gradient Recovery Method for Electron Magnetohydrodynamics with Fractional Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19716)
*Hailong Guo,Ruimeng Hu,Qirui Peng,Xu Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种面向2.5D电子磁流体动力学系统的保结构数值方法，采用分数阶耗散、梯度恢复算子、显式Hall积分因子和谱Hodge投影，实现了二阶空间收敛和稳定的Hall驱动动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对2.5D电子磁流体动力学系统，现有数值方法在保持系统内在结构（如能量稳定性、散度约束）方面存在不足，需要一种既能精确处理分数阶耗散又能保持离散能量稳定的高效数值格式。

Method: 1) 直接对磁场分量进行离散，结合梯度恢复算子；2) 采用半隐式保结构格式实现离散能量稳定性；3) 引入显式Hall积分因子实现高效计算；4) 在Fourier空间精确处理分数阶耗散；5) 通过谱Hodge投影强制满足面内散度约束。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在多个基准测试中实现了二阶空间收敛性和稳定的Hall驱动动力学行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的保结构数值方法在保持离散能量稳定性的同时，能够高效、精确地模拟2.5D电子磁流体动力学系统中的分数阶耗散和Hall效应，具有良好的收敛性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a structure-preserving numerical method for the $2\tfrac{1}{2}$-dimensional (2.5D) electron magnetohydrodynamics system with fractional dissipation on the periodic torus. The method works directly with the magnetic field components and combines this component formulation with the gradient recovery operator of [T. Chu, H. Guo, and Z. Zhang, SIAM J. Numer. Anal., 63 (2025), pp. 23--53]. We establish discrete energy stability for a semi-implicit structure-preserving formulation and use an explicit-Hall integrating-factor implementation for efficient computation on periodic grids. The fractional dissipation is treated exactly in Fourier space, and the in-plane divergence constraint is enforced by a spectral Hodge projection. Numerical experiments demonstrate second-order spatial convergence and stable Hall-driven dynamics across several benchmark tests.

</details>


### [124] [Well-balanced second-order approximation of the compressible atmospheric Euler equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19764)
*Crystal Farris,Matthias Maier,Eric J. Tovar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对含重力可压缩大气Euler方程的二阶近似方法，该方法具有保不变域和静力平衡状态保持特性，通过密度静力重构得到的辅助离散状态实现数值方法的稳定性和精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决含重力可压缩大气Euler方程数值求解中，同时满足保不变域和静力平衡状态保持（well-balanced）这一双重挑战。

Method: 基于密度静力重构（hydrostatic reconstruction）构建辅助离散状态，并结合数值状态的仿射偏移（affine shift）来提供局部界，从而维持方法的静力平衡保持和保不变域性质。

Result: 通过解析解、静力平衡测试以及典型大气流动基准问题对数值方法进行了验证和确认。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理含重力可压缩大气Euler方程，同时在保不变域和静力平衡保持方面表现良好。

Abstract: We introduce a second-order approximation to the compressible atmospheric Euler equations with gravity that is invariant domain preserving and well-balanced with respect to rest states. The approximation is built upon discrete auxiliary states derived from a hydrostatic reconstruction of the density. These auxiliary states, together with an affine shift of the numerical state, provide local bounds needed for maintaining well-balancing and invariant domain preserving properties of the method. The numerical method is then verified and validated with analytic solutions, well-balancing tests, and typical benchmark problems for atmospheric flows.

</details>


### [125] [Invariant measures of the stochastic theta method for stochastic differential equations with super-linearly growing coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19886)
*Xiaotong Li,Wei Liu,Wenjie Xiao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对漂移和扩散系数均可能超线性增长随机微分方程，提出使用随机theta方法逼近其不变测度，证明了数值不变测度的存在唯一性及其对精确不变测度的收敛性，并给出了数值模拟验证。该工作是现有结果在超线性扩散系数情形下的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有文献中随机theta方法逼近随机微分方程不变测度的结果，从仅漂移系数超线性增长的情形推广到漂移和扩散系数均超线性增长的情形。

Method: 采用随机theta方法（theta方法）对随机微分方程进行离散，通过分析数值解的遍历性，建立数值不变测度的存在唯一性，并利用数值方法的弱收敛性质证明数值不变测度对精确不变测度的收敛性。

Result: 证明了在漂移和扩散系数均超线性增长条件下，随机theta方法生成的数值不变测度存在唯一，且收敛到原SDE的精确不变测度。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将Jiang等人(2020)关于随机theta方法的结果从超线性漂移系数情形推广到超线性扩散系数情形，同时将Liu等人(2023)关于向后Euler-Maruyama方法的结果推广到随机theta方法。

Abstract: The stochastic theta method is proposed to approximate invariant measures of stochastic differential equations (SDEs), both of whose drift and diffusion coefficients may grow super-linearly. For the numerical solution generated by the stochastic theta method, we show the existence and uniqueness of the numerical invariant measure first. Then, we prove that the numerical invariant measure is convergent to the exact invariant measure of the underlying SDE. We also provide some numerical simulations to illustrate our theoretical results. This work could be regarded as an extension of the results in [Y. Jiang et al, Numer. Algorithms 83(4)(2020), pp. 1531-1553] to the case of super-linearly growing diffusion coefficient. As the backward Euler-Maruyama (EM) method is a special case of the stochastic theta method, the results derived in this work could also be regarded as a generalization of the results for the backward EM method in [W. Liu et al. Appl. Numer. Math. 184(2023), pp. 137-150] to the stochastic theta method.

</details>


### [126] [A fast direct solver based neural network for solving PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19895)
*Jashwanth Reddy Kadaru,Vaishnavi Gujjula*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HODLR矩阵快速直接求解器的神经网络架构，用于学习HODLR矩阵的逆运算，并通过将部分线性层替换为深度子网络，扩展为学习PDE非线性解算子。实验涵盖Fredholm积分方程、非线性薛定谔方程、Burgers方程、稳态达西流方程等，验证了其泛化能力、推理速度优势及与现有神经算子网络的对比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模N体问题产生的矩阵可用层次矩阵高效表示，其中可容许的离对角子矩阵可通过低秩矩阵近似。本文旨在利用神经网络学习HODLR矩阵的逆运算，并进一步扩展到PDE非线性解算子的学习，以提高求解效率。

Method: 基于Ambikasaran和Darve (2013)的HODLR矩阵快速直接求解器，设计神经网络学习其逆运算；并通过将部分线性层替换为深度子网络，扩展架构以学习PDE非线性解算子。

Result: 在线性问题（Fredholm积分方程）和PDE问题（非线性薛定谔方程、Burgers方程、稳态达西流方程）上验证了性能；展示了跨参数泛化能力；推理时间优于经典数值求解器；与现有神经算子网络进行了对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经网络架构能有效学习HODLR矩阵逆运算及PDE非线性解算子，在效率和泛化性上具有优势。

Abstract: The matrices arising from large scale $N$-body problems can be efficiently represented using hierarchical matrices, whose key idea is that the admissible off-diagonal sub-matrices can be well approximated by low-rank matrices across a hierarchy of matrix partitions. HODLR (Hierarchical Off-Diagonal Low-Rank) matrices are a subclass of hierarchical matrices in which all off-diagonal submatrices at every level of a recursive binary partition are low-rank. In this article, we present a neural network that learns the inverse operation of HODLR matrices based on the fast direct solver for HODLR matrices developed by Ambikasaran and Darve (2013). We further extend the architecture to learn nonlinear solution operators associated with PDEs by replacing some of the linear layers with deep sub-networks. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed architecture by performing a comprehensive set of experiments that include (i) solving a linear problem such as the Fredholm integral equation of the second kind, (ii) solving PDEs such as the nonlinear Schrödinger equation, Burgers' equation, and the steady-state Darcy's flow equation, (iii) generalization study across varying parameter values, (iv) comparing the inference time of the proposed network with the run time of a classical numerical solver, and (v) comparing the proposed network with some of the existing neural operator learning networks.

</details>


### [127] [Structure-Oriented Randomized Neural Networks for Poisson-Nernst-Planck and Poisson-Nernst-Planck-Navier-Stokes Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19912)
*Yunlong Li,Fei Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向PNP和PNP-NS系统的结构导向随机神经网络框架(SO-RaNN)，通过解耦线性化子问题的随机神经网络迭代求解，结合点截断保正、离散质量缩放因子校正、SAV后处理修正以及速度场的结构保持随机神经网络(SP-RaNN)，实现了物理约束下的数值求解，并给出了理论误差分析与收敛性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNP和PNP-NS系统在离子输运、电化学等领域有重要应用，传统数值方法在复杂几何和高维问题中面临挑战。随机神经网络提供了一种无网格求解方案，但如何保证解的非负性、质量守恒、能量耗散等物理结构性质是关键难题。

Method: 提出SO-RaNN框架：(1)采用空间-时间框架下的随机神经网络迭代求解解耦线性化子问题；(2)对浓度变量实施点截断保正，在选定校正时刻计算离散质量缩放因子并进行时间插值，实现精确质量匹配与近似质量守恒；(3)引入SAV后处理修正以提供辅助离散耗散机制；(4)对PNP-NS系统采用SP-RaNN近似速度场，通过构造满足逐点不可压缩约束。

Result: 推导了未校正RaNN求解器的残差估计、PNP系统原始Picard迭代的条件局部时间收敛性结果，以及值层保正修正、质量校正和SAV后处理的理论分析。对PNP-NS系统证明了SP-RaNN空间的逼近性质并给出了线性化Oseen型问题的条件误差估计。数值实验验证了源驱动制造解测试中的逼近精度，以及保正修正、选定时刻质量匹配、自由能曲线计算和散度自由逼近的预期效果。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将随机神经网络与物理结构保持技术相结合，为PNP及PNP-NS系统提供了一种兼具理论保证和数值精度的无网格求解方法。

Abstract: We develop a structure-oriented randomized neural network framework, termed SO-RaNN, for the Poisson-Nernst-Planck (PNP) system and the Poisson-Nernst-Planck-Navier-Stokes (PNP-NS) system. The decoupled linearized subproblems are solved iteratively by randomized neural networks in a space-time framework. For the concentration variables, a pointwise cut-off is used to enforce positivity at the value level, and discrete mass-scaling factors are computed at selected correction instants and interpolated in time, so as to ensure exact mass matching at those instants and to promote approximate mass preservation between them. To introduce an auxiliary discrete dissipation mechanism, we further employ an SAV-type post-processing correction, which yields monotonicity of the SAV auxiliary variable under the ideal SAV update. For the PNP-NS system, a structure-preserving randomized neural network (SP-RaNN) is used for the velocity field, so that the velocity approximation satisfies the incompressibility constraint pointwise by construction. On the theoretical side, we derive residual-based estimates for the raw, uncorrected RaNN solvers of the linearized subproblems, formulate a conditional local-in-time convergence result for the raw outer Picard iteration of the PNP system, and analyze the value-level positivity correction together with the mass-correction and SAV post-processing steps. For the PNP-NS system, we establish an approximation result for the SP-RaNN space and provide a conditional error statement for the corresponding linearized Oseen-type problem. Numerical experiments demonstrate approximation accuracy in the source-driven manufactured tests and illustrate the intended value-level positivity correction, selected-time mass matching, computed free-energy curves based on the final gauge-fixed potential, and divergence-free approximation in benchmark tests.

</details>


### [128] [A posteriori error bounds for pseudo-parabolic equations using $C_0$ semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20073)
*Martin Ossadnik,Torsten Linß*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对伪抛物型偏微分方程，推导了空间有限元法和时间BDF格式耦合求解的后验误差界。分析基于$C_0$半群理论和椭圆重构概念的适应性改进，并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为伪抛物型PDE的数值求解提供严格的后验误差分析框架。这类方程在物理和工程中具有重要应用，但此前缺乏系统的后验误差界理论。

Method: 采用$C_0$半群理论进行时间连续性分析，将椭圆重构方法推广到伪抛物问题，结合空间FEM离散和时间BDF格式，推导后验误差上界。

Result: 成功建立了伪抛物型PDE的全离散FEM-BDF格式的后验误差界，数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法为伪抛物问题提供了可靠的后验误差估计，拓展了椭圆重构技术的适用范围，可用于指导自适应网格加密等计算优化。

Abstract: A class of pseudo-parabolic partial differential equations is considered. We derive a posteriori error bounds for approximations obtained by FEMs in space and a BDF formula in time. The analysis is based on the $C_0$ semigroup theory and an adaptation of the concept of elliptic reconstruction to pseudo-parabolic problems. The analysis is complemented with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [129] [A conservative adaptive rank method for the Wigner-Poisson system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20234)
*Andrew Christlieb,Sining Gong,F. Alejandro Padilla-Gomez,Jing-Mei Qiu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于1D1V Wigner-Poisson系统的保守自适应秩方法，通过结合基于采样的自适应秩更新与保守宏观修正（包括局部分布-动量修正、Fermi-Dirac型重构和全局二次矩修正），在保持离散守恒量的同时实现相空间演化的降维，数值实验验证了该方法在多种量子参数下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低确定性量子动力学模拟中相空间演化的计算成本，同时保持物理保真度所需的宏观不变量（质量、动量、能量）。

Method: 将基于采样的自适应秩Wigner-Poisson更新与保守宏观修正相结合：采用保守的密度-动量求解提供局域宏观更新，Fermi-Dirac型重构将宏观信息传递至动力学解，全局二次矩修正强制执行离散总能量约束，最后通过ACA SVD表示更新状态以实现数值秩的自适应调整。

Result: 在双流不稳定性、强Landau阻尼和bump-on-tail不稳定性等数值实验中，该方法能够捕捉基准Wigner-Poisson动力学，维持有界自适应秩，且全局离散不变量守恒误差接近机器精度。与全局保守公式的对比表明两种修正策略在周期性测试中结果几乎一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的保守自适应秩方法能有效处理Wigner-Poisson系统，在降低计算代价的同时保持物理守恒性，且Fermi-Dirac型修正更符合该模型的量子统计结构。

Abstract: We propose a conservative adaptive rank method for the 1D1V Wigner-Poisson system. The method targets a central challenge in deterministic quantum kinetic simulations: reducing the cost of phase-space evolution while preserving the macroscopic invariants needed for physical fidelity. The scheme combines a sampling-based adaptive rank Wigner-Poisson update [7] with a conservative macroscopic correction. A conservative density-momentum solve provides local macroscopic updates, a Fermi-Dirac-type reconstruction transfers them to the kinetic solution, and a global quadratic moment correction enforces the discrete total energy constraint at the kinetic level. Unlike Maxwell-Boltzmann-type corrections commonly used in classical kinetic settings, the reconstruction uses a Fermi-Dirac-type form motivated by the model's quantum-statistical structure. The corrected state is incorporated into an ACA SVD representation, allowing the numerical rank to adapt to the phase-space complexity generated by the nonlocal Wigner operator and self-consistent Poisson field. Numerical experiments for the two-stream instability, strong Landau damping, and bump-on tail instability show that the method captures benchmark Wigner-Poisson dynamics for several values of the quantum parameter H, maintains bounded adaptive ranks, and preserves the specified global discrete invariants with conservation errors near machine precision. We also compare this formulation, which uses local density-momentum correction plus global total energy correction, with a related globally conservative formulation for mass, momentum, and energy [8]. The two approaches produce nearly identical phase-space and diagnostic results for the periodic benchmark test considered here, indicating that both correction strategies are compatible with adaptive rank compression for Wigner-Poisson dynamics in the tested 1D1V periodic setting.

</details>


### [130] [Data dependent Shepard approximation through and adaptive modification of the shape parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20332)
*José Kuruc,Juan Ruiz-Álvarez,Bo Wang,Dionisio-Félix Yáñez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据依赖的Shepard插值方法，通过自适应调整影响权重（基于局部光滑性指标修改形状参数），在保持光滑区域高精度的同时，有效减少一维和二维数据中跳变不连续处的模糊现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Shepard插值在数据存在跳变不连续时会产生过度模糊（smearing），而[2]中的自适应策略虽能解决RBF插值中的类似问题，但尚未被推广到Shepard框架。本文旨在弥补这一空白，将自适应思想扩展到Shepard插值中，以降低不连续处的模糊。

Method: 借鉴[2]的自适应策略，在经典Shepard插值中引入数据依赖的权重调整机制：对网格数据使用平方未分割二阶差分，对散乱数据使用平方最小二乘Laplacian近似（按局部平均分离尺度缩放）作为光滑性指标，动态修改形状参数，使靠近不连续处的核函数表现为局部delta函数。

Result: 理论证明了新插值方法的性质以及减少不连续处模糊的可能性。一维和二维数值实验表明，该方法在光滑区域保持高精度的同时，显著降低了跳变不连续处的模糊现象。

Conclusion: 提出的数据依赖Shepard插值有效解决了经典Shepard方法在跳变不连续处过度模糊的问题，兼具理论保证与实验验证，为处理含间断的数据插值问题提供了新方案。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce a novel data-dependent Shepard interpolation method inspired by the adaptive strategies proposed in [2]. In this case, as Shepard interpolation does not produce oscillations, our approach has the core objective of reducing the smearing near jump discontinuities in the data in one and two dimensions. While the original work in [2] focuses in on Radial Basis Function (RBF) interpolation, we extend these ideas to the Shepard framework by incorporating a data-dependent adaptation mechanism. Specifically, we modify the classical Shepard interpolation by adaptively adjusting the influence weights based on local smoothness indicators that modify the shape parameter. These indicators, similar to those used in [2], are designed to detect discontinuities: for grid-based data, we use squared undivided second-order differences, and for scattered data, we employ squared least-squares approximations of the Laplacian scaled by the square of the mean local separation of stencil points. The resulting data-dependent weighting scheme forces the kernels close to a discontinuity to behave like a local delta function, effectively reducing the smearing of the discontinuities introduced by the classical Shepard approach. We establish the theoretical foundation of the method, including the properties of the new interpolation and we theoretically prove that the reduction of the smearing of discontinuities is possible. Numerical experiments in one and two dimensions confirm that the proposed data-dependent Shepard interpolation significantly reduces the smearing of jump discontinuities while maintaining high accuracy in smooth regions.

</details>


### [131] [Nonlinear Geotechnical Analysis Using a Polygonal Cell-Based Smoothed Finite Element Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20384)
*Mingjiao Yan,Yang Yang,Zongliang Zhang,Yinpeng Yin,Miao Zhang,Yijia Dong,Dong Pan,Xiaozi Lin,Tiankai Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多边形单元的光滑有限元法（CS-FEM），用于非线性岩土工程分析，并通过ABAQUS用户子程序实现。该方法结合Wachspress插值和单元应变光滑技术，避免了多边形单元内形函数导数的直接计算，可统一处理标准多边形网格和含悬挂节点的混合四叉树网格。通过多个基准和工程算例验证，该方法在位移、应力、塑性应变、承载力及安全系数预测方面具有较高精度，同时提升了网格灵活性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性岩土分析常涉及复杂几何、分期施工、局部破坏以及网格依赖的应力和塑性应变响应，现有数值方法在处理这些问题时存在局限性，需要开发一种兼具网格灵活性和计算效率的新方法。

Method: 采用Wachspress插值构建多边形单元形函数，结合基于单元的光滑应变技术，通过多边形光滑子单元的边界积分计算光滑应变-位移矩阵，避免直接求导；通过增量弹塑性本构更新（Mohr-Coulomb模型和Duncan-Chang模型）纳入非线性岩土材料行为，并在ABAQUS中通过用户单元子程序实现。

Result: 通过开孔板、条形基础、心墙堆石坝、隧道开挖和边坡稳定等算例验证，该方法能准确预测位移、应力、塑性应变、承载力和安全系数，并展现出更优的网格适应性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的多边形CS-FEM方法为非线性岩土工程分析提供了一种高精度、高网格灵活性和良好计算效率的数值工具，统一了标准多边形网格与含悬挂节点的混合四叉树网格的处理框架。

Abstract: Nonlinear geotechnical analysis often involves complex geometries, staged construction, local failure, and mesh-dependent stress and plastic strain responses. This study develops a polygonal cell-based smoothed finite element method (CS-FEM) for nonlinear geotechnical analysis and implements it in ABAQUS through the user element subroutine. The proposed method combines Wachspress interpolation with cell-based strain smoothing, in which the smoothed strain--displacement matrix is evaluated by boundary integration over polygonal smoothing subcells. This formulation avoids direct calculation of shape-function derivatives inside polygonal elements and enables standard polygonal meshes and hybrid quadtree meshes with hanging nodes to be handled in a unified framework. Nonlinear geomaterial behavior is incorporated through incremental elasto-plastic constitutive updates, including the Mohr--Coulomb model and the Duncan--Chang model. Several benchmark and engineering examples, including a perforated plate, strip footing, core rockfill dam, tunnel excavation, and slope stability problems, are presented for verification. The results show that the proposed method accurately predicts displacement, stress, plastic strain, bearing capacity, and factor of safety, while providing improved mesh flexibility and computational efficiency for nonlinear geotechnical analysis.

</details>


### [132] [CoarseSolvers for Exascale Solution of Poisson Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20496)
*Thilina Ratnayaka,Paul Fischer,Luke Olson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两层Schwarz方法作为p-多重网格预条件子中粗层求解器（替代代数多重网格AMG），用于求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程离散化产生的压力Poisson方程。该方法包含局部问题和全局粗问题，核心贡献是设计了结构化的非嵌套粗空间，实现了无通信插值。在Summit/Frontier超算上的Nek5000/RS数值实验证明了其优于BoomerAMG求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有p-多重网格预条件子中使用AMG作为粗层求解器存在通信开销大、可扩展性受限的问题，需要一种更高效的替代方案来提升大规模并行计算中压力Poisson方程的求解效率。

Method: 提出两层Schwarz方法替代AMG作为pMG粗层求解器：第一层解决原始pMG粗空间中的局部问题；第二层构建一个结构化的、非嵌套的全局粗问题。该全局粗空间的结构化特性使得插值操作无需通信。

Result: 在Summit/Frontier超算上使用Nek5000/RS求解器进行系列实验，证明了所提方法在求解效率上优于当前最先进的AMG求解器BoomerAMG。

Conclusion: 提出的结构化非嵌套粗空间Schwarz方法能有效替代AMG作为pMG粗层求解器，其通信自由的插值特性使其在大规模并行环境中具有显著优势。

Abstract: WepresentatwolevelSchwarzmethodasanalternativetoAlgebraicMultigridmethod(AMG) used as the last level (coarse) solver of the p-multigrid pMG preconditioner for pressure Poission equation resulting from Spectral/Finite element descretization of incompressible Navier-Stokes eqaution. Proposed Schwarz method consits of a local problem in the original pMG coarse space and a global coarse problem. Main contribution of the paper is a novel, structured and a non-nested coarse space for the global coarse problem. Structured nature of the proposed global coarse space enable communication-free interpolation between the original p-multgrid coarse space and the global coarse problem. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method compared to the state of the art AMG solver BoomerAMG by a series of experiments performed using Nek5000/RS, a suite of highly scalable incompressible Navier-Stokes solvers, on Summit/Frontier supercomputers at Oak Ridge Leadership Computing Facility.

</details>
