### [133] [Scintillation of the first-known pulsar planetary system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19406)
*J. M. Yao,L. Zhang,A. Wolszczan,William A. Coles,D. Li,Richard N. Manchester,N. Wang,C. H. Niu,P. Wang,F. F. Kou,J. P. Yuan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用FAST望远镜对首颗脉冲星行星系统PSR B1257+12进行闪烁研究，分析了31次观测数据，测量了闪烁时标、带宽和频率漂移率，发现了次级谱中的内、中、外弧结构，证实了各向同性散射，确定了散射屏距离和速度，并结合长期计时数据分析发现色散量变化主要受远离脉冲星的等离子体主导，表明脉冲星周围环境可能较为洁净。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过对首颗脉冲星行星系统PSR B1257+12的星际闪烁研究，探测其星际介质的结构与性质，并结合色散量变化分析脉冲星周围环境，以理解该系统的星际介质特性及脉冲星的局部环境状况。

Method: 利用FAST望远镜进行31次（≥30分钟）观测，对14次长时（≥120分钟）观测做一维自相关函数分析测量闪烁参数；通过次级谱分析探测弧结构；利用年调制分析内弧曲率确定散射屏距离和速度；通过时延轮廓分析外弧和内弧的谱指数；结合长期计时数据对比色散量变化。

Result: 12个历元测得闪烁时标、带宽和频率漂移率；两个观测因天文单位尺度结构干扰无法测量；三个观测中同时检测到内、中、外弧；内弧年调制表明各向同性散射，屏距离233±28 pc，横向速度V_scr,α=-7.16±2.16 km/s，V_scr,δ=-41.07±5.69 km/s；内、外弧谱指数≤Kolmogorov值；中、外弧屏-脉冲星距离分别为354±22 pc和166±12 pc；DM变化主要受远离脉冲星的等离子体主导；外弧DM变化率低，脉冲星周围可能较洁净。

Conclusion: PSR B1257+12的星际散射呈现各向同性特征，存在多尺度散射屏结构；脉冲星周围环境相对洁净；低DM变化率支持电磁波传播路径上的散射主要来自远处星际介质；但也不能排除存在更靠近脉冲星的未探测散射屏，需要更高灵敏度或更长时观测。

Abstract: We present a scintillation study of the first-known pulsar planetary system, PSR~B1257+12, using the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST). A total of 31 observations with durations greater than or equal to 30 minutes were analyzed. For 14 longer observations (greater than or equal to 120 minutes), one-dimensional autocorrelation function analyses yielded the scintillation timescale, scintillation bandwidth, and frequency-drift rate for 12 epochs. Two observations show strong periodic modulation in the frequency-domain auto-correlation function, likely caused by astronomical-unit-scale structures along the propagation path, preventing reliable measurements of the scintillation timescale and bandwidth. In three observations, secondary spectra reveal simultaneous detections of inner, middle, and outer arcs. Analysis of the annual modulation of the inner-arc curvature indicates isotropic scattering, with a screen distance of $233\pm28$~pc and transverse velocity $V_{\rm scr,α}=-7.16\pm2.16$ km~s$^{-1}$, $V_{\rm scr,δ}=-41.07\pm5.69$ km~s$^{-1}$. Delay-profile analysis for both the inner and outer arcs suggest spectral exponents consistent with, or smaller than, the Kolmogorov value. Under isotropic scattering, the screen--pulsar distances are $354\pm22$~pc and $166\pm12$~pc for the middle and outer arcs. Combining the results from long-term timing analyses with our scintillation measurements, we find that the dispersion measure (DM) variations are primarily dominated by plasma located further away from the pulsar. The low DM-change rate of the outer arc and the absence of nearby scattering screens suggest that the immediate environment of the pulsar may be relatively clean. Alternatively, scattering screens closer to the pulsar may exist but remain undetected, requiring higher-sensitivity or longer-duration observations.

</details>


### [134] [Evidence for protostellar jets as a population of hadronic gamma-ray sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19445)
*Javier Méndez-Gallego,Rubén López-Coto,Emma de Oña Wilhelmi,Stefano Menchiari,Iván Agudo,Rubén Fedriani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了在年轻恒星天体（原恒星）中统计显著探测到伽马射线辐射，揭示了一类新的银河系“伽马亮原恒星”。研究表明，原恒星喷流中的质子被加速并与周围分子云相互作用，通过π⁰介子衰变产生伽马射线。宇宙线输出与辐射光度相关，表明粒子加速与系统的机械功率成比例。该发现为非热过程在原恒星演化中的作用打开了新的观测窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，原恒星仅被视为热辐射源，但射电同步辐射喷流暗示了相对论性电子的存在；然而，质子加速的直接证据一直缺失。本文旨在通过伽马射线观测确认原恒星喷流中的质子加速现象。

Method: 对年轻恒星天体样本进行伽马射线统计显著性探测，分析伽马射线发射与辐射光度之间的相关性，通过π⁰介子衰变机制解释观测到的伽马射线辐射。

Result: 首次统计显著探测到来自原恒星群体的伽马射线辐射，发现宇宙线输出与辐射光度呈相关性，证明粒子加速效率与系统机械功率成正比。

Conclusion: 原恒星喷流中存在高效的质子加速过程，非热过程在原恒星演化中具有重要作用；伽马射线研究为理解吸积、喷流反馈以及恒星和行星形成环境提供了关键观测手段。

Abstract: Stars are born in darkness, deep within cold, dense molecular clouds where gravity drives the collapse of gas and dust, giving rise to protostars, the earliest stages of stellar evolution. Once considered purely thermal sources, these young systems are now emerging as sites of energetic non-thermal activity. While radio synchrotron jets hinted at the presence of relativistic electrons, direct confirmation of proton acceleration remained elusive. Here we report a statistically significant detection of gamma rays from a population of young stellar objects, revealing a Galactic class of Gamma-Loud Protostars. Observations point towards particle acceleration within protostellar jets, where gamma-ray emission arises from protons interacting with surrounding molecular clouds via pion decay. We find a correlation between cosmic-ray output and bolometric luminosity, suggesting that particle acceleration scales with the system's mechanical power. These findings open a new observational window into the role of non-thermal processes in protostellar evolution and suggest that gamma-ray studies of protostars can provide critical insights into accretion, ejection, and feedback in star formation. This previously overlooked emission traces the energetic feedback that young stars inject into their surroundings, shaping the conditions for subsequent star and planet formation.

</details>


### [135] [Damping of Fast Radio Bursts in the Inner Magnetospheres of Magnetars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19448)
*Siddhant Solanki,Jens Mahlmann,Alexander Philippov,Andrei Beloborodov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过三维力自由电动力学模拟，研究了快速射电暴（FRB）在磁星磁层中的传播机制，发现FRB会激发阿尔文涨落，导致强烈的非线性衰减。在静态偶极磁层中，非线性衰减在10-100磁星半径范围内有效，之后因电荷匮乏而停止；在磁层爆发期的相对论性外流中，三波相互作用保持高效，将爆发性FRB的逃逸半径约束在10^2-10^3磁星半径以上。结论证实非线性等离子体过程强烈限制了FRB从磁星内磁层的逃逸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解FRB在磁星磁层中的传播机制，探究非线性等离子体过程对FRB逃逸的限制作用。

Method: 使用三维力自由电动力学（force-free electrodynamics）数值模拟。

Result: 在静态偶极磁层中，非线性衰减在10-100个磁星半径内有效，更大半径处因电荷匮乏而停止；在磁层爆发期的相对论性外流中，三波相互作用保持高效，将FRB逃逸半径约束在10^2-10^3磁星半径以上。

Conclusion: 非线性等离子体过程强烈限制了FRB从磁星内磁层的逃逸。

Abstract: We investigate the propagation of fast radio bursts (FRBs) through magnetar magnetospheres. Previous work showed that, in the inner magnetosphere, GHz radio waves propagate as fast magnetosonic waves and undergo resonant three-wave interactions that transfer their energy into trapped Alfvén waves. Using three-dimensional force-free electrodynamics simulations, we demonstrate that FRBs would excite Alfvénic fluctuations, leading to strong nonlinear attenuation of the radio signal. In quiescent dipolar magnetospheres, the nonlinear decay stays efficient within $\sim10$--$100$ magnetar radii; charge starvation of the excited Alfvén waves stops the decay at larger radii. For FRBs propagating within relativistic magnetic outflows launched during magnetospheric eruptions, three-wave interactions remain efficient and constrain the escape radius to $\gtrsim10^2$--$10^3$ magnetar radii for luminous bursts. Our results confirm that nonlinear plasma processes strongly limit the escape of FRBs from the inner magnetospheres of magnetars.

</details>


### [136] [Nonlinear Decay of Fast Magnetosonic Waves through Weak Turbulence: Force-Free Electrodynamics Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19450)
*Siddhant Solanki,Jens Mahlmann,Alexander Philippov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过相对论力-电力学模拟研究磁星磁层中低频快磁声波（FMS）的传播，发现FMS波通过参量衰变不稳定性有效非线性转化为次级FMS波和Alfvén波，即使在能量均分后仍持续耗散能量，产生宽谱激发，表明FMS波难以无耗散地逃逸磁星磁层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究磁星内磁层产生的GHz快速射电暴（FRB）的低频快磁声波能否在不经历显著再处理的情况下逃逸，这是一个未解决的关键问题。

Method: 采用相对论力-电力学模拟（relativistic force-free electrodynamics simulations）验证Golbraikh & Lyubarsky (2023)的理论预测。

Result: FMS波通过参量衰变不稳定性高效非线性转化为次级FMS波和Alfvén波；能量在FMS与Alfvén分量之间达到近似均分后，耗散过程仍在持续；激发波的频谱很宽，覆盖模拟域中惯性范围的大部分k空间。

Conclusion: FMS波在磁星磁层中难以逃逸，会经历显著的耗散和频谱展宽。

Abstract: We investigate the propagation of low-frequency fast-magnetosonic (FMS) waves in highly magnetized environments. Such conditions are relevant to the escape of GHz fast radio bursts potentially produced in the inner magnetospheres of magnetars. It remains an open question whether such waves can escape without substantial reprocessing. Using relativistic force-free electrodynamics simulations, we confirm the key theoretical predictions of Golbraikh & Lyubarsky (2023) and demonstrate that FMS waves undergo efficient nonlinear conversion into secondary FMS and Alfvén waves via the parametric decay instability. This process continues to drain energy from the primary FMS waves even after approximate energy equipartition between the FMS and Alfvén components is established. The resulting spectrum of excited waves is broad, extending across much of the inertial range in $k$-space within the simulation domain. Our results indicate that FMS waves likely do not escape magnetar magnetospheres without substantial dissipation and spectral broadening.

</details>


### [137] [Short-Range Forces Can Catalyze Extreme Orbital Evolution in Hierarchical Triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19456)
*Ygal Y. Klein,Chris Hamilton*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文发现，在分层三体系统中，当放宽双平均近似时，短程力（如相对论、潮汐/自转隆起）非但不会抑制von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai效应导致的高偏心率激发，反而会通过非绝热跃迁催化极端偏心率行为，使双星系统探索比现有半解析ZLK理论更广的相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论认为短程力引起的近星点进动会抑制ZLK效应的高偏心率激发，但该结论基于双平均近似。作者想探究在放松该近似后，短程力对极端偏心率行为的真实影响。

Method: 在测试粒子四极矩层次下，放松双平均近似，数值研究短程力（相对论、潮汐/自转隆起）对系统长期动力学演化的影响，分析非绝热跃迁过程及角动量分量的演化。

Result: 短程力可在高偏心率阶段驱动双星有效"绝热不变量"的离散跳跃，导致最大/最小偏心率和长期周期发生显著变化；角动量分量$j_z$出现长期演化——这是首次在四极矩阶三体机制中被证明。

Conclusion: 短程力在放松双平均近似时催化而非抑制极端偏心率行为，双星系统可探索比现有半解析ZLK理论更广的相空间，角动量$j_z$也存在长期演化。

Abstract: Hierarchical triples are promising environments for producing exotica such as black hole mergers and hot Jupiters, because of the von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai (ZLK) effect, whereby a distant tertiary can torque an inner binary to high eccentricity over secular timescales. In the double-averaged (DA) approximation to ZLK, this eccentricity excitation is suppressed by apsidal precession due to `short-range forces' (SRFs) like relativity and tidal/rotational bulges. Here we show that, when the DA approximation is relaxed, SRFs often catalyze, rather than suppress, extreme eccentricity behavior. This occurs because SRFs can drive large, discrete jumps in the binary's effective `adiabatic invariants' during high-eccentricity episodes. These nonadiabatic jumps can dramatically alter the maximum/minimum eccentricity and secular period of astrophysically relevant triples, including some for which SRFs were previously thought irrelevant. Even the angular momentum component $j_z$ evolves secularly -- to our knowledge, this is the first time such evolution has been demonstrated from a quadrupole-order, three-body mechanism. In short, binaries may explore much more of phase space than is implied by any (semi-)analytic ZLK theory of which we are aware. We demonstrate this at the test-particle quadrupole level; in a companion paper we show how even more-extreme behavior occurs when the jumps are combined with octupolar ZLK evolution.

</details>


### [138] [On the Contribution of Local Sources to the Galactic Cosmic-Ray Spectrum: An Exact Series Solution for Two-Zone Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19701)
*Zi-Hang Liu,Yiwei Bao,Ruo-Yu Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究宇宙线质子和氦谱在膝以下偏离简单幂律的现象。通过蒙特卡洛模拟发现，考虑近源慢扩散（双区模型）后，局部最强源贡献与背景相当的几率从0.4%升至1.7-2.2%，但统计困难并未消除。进一步分析候选超新星遗迹表明，10 TeV特征仅在额外假设（硬注入谱、有利扩散系数）下可重现，且不同传输模型预测差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙线质子和氦谱在膝以下（多TeV区域）出现偏离简单幂律的结构，一种解释是附近单个或多个源贡献了额外成分。但此前同质扩散模型研究表明局部源主导的可能性极低。本文旨在探究若考虑近源非均匀扩散（慢扩散区域），这一统计概率如何变化。

Method: 推导了双区扩散模型的级联Green函数，实现快速计算粒子分布；基于此进行银河系源种群的蒙特卡洛模拟，比较同质扩散与双区模型（近源慢扩散）下局部最强源贡献的统计概率；并检验已知邻近超新星遗迹候选者能否解释10 TeV特征。

Result: 双区模型中，局部最强源贡献与背景可比（10 TeV）的概率从同质扩散的约0.4%提升至1.7-2.2%。但即便考虑近源慢扩散，统计困难仅被削弱而未消除。已知候选超新星遗迹需额外假设（更硬注入谱、有利扩散系数）才能复现10 TeV特征，且不同传输模型预测差异显著。

Conclusion: 局部源解释虽具可能性，但高度依赖模型，需独立约束源注入历史、粒子传输机制及局部星际湍流。慢扩散区域虽能提高概率，但不足以解决统计困难。

Abstract: Measurements of cosmic-ray proton and helium spectra below the knee show deviations from simple power laws, including multi-TeV structures. A possible explanation is that one or a few nearby sources contribute an additional component to the local spectrum. However, previous study shows that a dominant local contribution is statistically unlikely under a homogeneous diffusion model. In this work, we investigate how this probability changes if cosmic rays experience inefficient transport near their sources, motivated by observations of extended gamma-ray emission around Galactic accelerators. We derive a series Green's function that enables fast calculation of the particle distribution in this scenario, making Monte Carlo calculations for Galactic source populations feasible. The inner slow-diffusion region delays escape and redistributes the arriving particles in time and energy. In Monte Carlo realizations, the probability that the strongest local source becomes comparable to the background at $10\,\rm{TeV}$ increases from about $0.4\%$ in homogeneous diffusion to $1.7$--$2.2\%$ in the two-zone models. Thus inhibited near-source transport weakens, but does not remove, the statistical difficulty. We then examine cataloged nearby candidate supernova remnants and show that a $10\,\rm{TeV}$ feature can be reproduced only with additional assumptions, especially a harder local injection spectrum and a favorable diffusion coefficient. The predicted contribution of a given source changes strongly among different particle transport model. Therefore, the local source interpretations are plausible but highly model dependent, and require independent constraints on source injection history, particle transport mechanisms, and local interstellar turbulence.

</details>


### [139] [On the braking index evolution of PSR B0540-69: wind braking of pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19856)
*H. Tong*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究脉冲星PSR B0450-69的制动指数演化，发现其在高自转减慢态下的制动指数随时间增加并趋近先前预测值，提出粒子风模型解释瞬态变化，最终制动指数呈指数形式演化并趋于稳定值，同时给出脉冲星风制动模型的唯象处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释PSR B0450-69在高自转减慢态下制动指数随时间增加的现象，验证先前提出的粒子风增强导致自转减慢态变化的预测，并探索制动指数演化的物理机制。

Method: 观测数据分析PSR B0450-69制动指数的时间演化；对比实测制动指数与理论预测值；提出粒子数密度微小变化解释制动指数瞬态变化；建立制动指数指数演化模型；给出风制动模型的唯象处理方法。

Result: 当前实测制动指数正趋近先前预测值；制动指数瞬态变化可由粒子数密度的微小变化解释；最终制动指数演化呈指数形式；PSR B0540-69的制动指数预期趋近某稳定值。

Conclusion: 未来制动指数测量可阐明制动指数演化的物理机制，检验脉冲星磁层中不同的粒子加速势模型；提出的唯象风制动模型可简化应用于脉冲星制动指数、间歇脉冲星及PSR B0540-69。

Abstract: The pulsar PSR B0450-69 has both a braking index measurement and spin-down state change. After its spin-down state change, it shows an increasing braking index with time. Previously, it is pointed out that the spin-down state change may be caused by an enhanced particle wind. The prediction is that its braking index in the high spin-down state will be smaller. The current measured braking index is approaching the previous prediction. The transient variation of braking index may be due to a small varying part of the particle number density. The final braking index evolution is found to be in an exponential form. The braking index of PSR B0540-69 is expected to approach some steady value. Future braking index measurement may make clear the physics for the braking index evolution. It may also help to make test different particle acceleration potential in the pulsar magnetosphere. Finally, a phenomenological treatment of wind braking model of pulsars is presented. It can simplify the applications to pulsar braking index, intermittent pulsars and PSR B0540-69.

</details>


### [140] [Accreting stellar-mass black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19952)
*Greg Marcel,Bailey Tetarenko,Adam Ingram,Tom Maccarone,Alexandra Veledina,Phil Charles*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了吸积恒星级黑洞的多波段观测进展，包括连续谱、时变特性、发射线和X射线偏振，揭示了吸积盘、日冕和喷流之间的复杂相互作用，并指出软态的偏振观测与理论预测不符，强调了未来多波段、时变和偏振研究的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星质量黑洞的吸积过程表现出从谱态转变、爆发到喷流产生的剧烈电磁波段变化，使其成为理解强引力场中吸积物理的独特实验室。当前对谱态转变的物理起源、磁场在驱动外流和塑造吸积流中的作用、以及盘不稳定性与喷流发射之间的联系仍存在根本性问题。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统整合了近年来在连续谱发射、时变性质（包括准周期振荡和宽带噪声）、多波段发射线诊断以及X射线偏振测量等方面的观测与理论研究进展。

Result: 日冕在硬态中可能垂直于喷流轴方向延展；软态的偏振观测与简单理论预期不符，对吸积几何的解释构成挑战；不同波段的发射线（尤其是铁K线）为内盘结构和相对论性反射提供了关键探针。

Conclusion: 尽管取得显著进展，谱态转变的物理机制、磁场在驱动外流和塑造吸积流中的作用、以及盘不稳定性与喷流发射的关联仍待解答；未来需要多波段、时变和偏振研究的协同推进。

Abstract: Accreting stellar-mass black holes exhibit dramatic variability across the electromagnetic spectrum, including spectral state transitions, outbursts, and jet production, making them unique laboratories for understanding accretion processes in strong gravitational fields. This review synthesizes recent progress in understanding these systems, focusing on their continuum emission, timing properties, emission lines, and X-ray polarization. A complex interplay between the accretion disk, the so-called corona, and jet underlies the observed spectral and timing behavior, with quasi-periodic oscillations and broadband noise providing windows into the dynamics of the innermost accretion flow. Emission lines across all wavelengths serve as critical diagnostics of disk structure, outflows, and reprocessing, while iron K lines in the X-ray band probe the properties of the inner disk through relativistic reflection. Polarization studies suggest that the corona is likely extended perpendicular to the jet axis in the hard state, while the soft state remains poorly understood, with observations that do not yet conform to simple theoretical expectations; a puzzle that continues to challenge our interpretation of accretion geometry. Despite significant advances, fundamental questions remain about the physical origins of state transitions, the role of magnetic fields in driving outflows and shaping the accretion flow, and the connection between disk instabilities and jet launching. This review underscores the need for future multi-wavelength, timing, and polarimetric studies to deepen our understanding of accretion physics in strong-gravity environments.

</details>


### [141] [Supernova Remnants in the IXPE era: a review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19979)
*Riccardo Ferrazzoli,IXPE Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: IXPE对五颗年轻超新星遗迹的X射线偏振观测揭示了显著偏振（5%-30%+），磁场的径向（Cas A、Tycho、SN 1006）与切向（RX J1713.7-3946、Vela Jr.）双重拓扑形态挑战了传统射电影像认知，表明激波速度与周围介质密度共同塑造磁场结构，为粒子加速机制提供了关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用IXPE提供的首个空间分辨X射线偏振测量，直接探测超新星遗迹中有效粒子加速区域的磁场几何与湍流状态，从而约束扩散激波加速和磁场放大模型。

Method: IXPE对六颗年轻超新星遗迹（Cas A、Tycho、SN 1006、RX J1713.7-3946、Vela Jr.、RCW 86）进行X射线偏振观测，其中前五颗已有发表结果，测量偏振度并分析磁场拓扑结构。

Result: 所有遗迹均探测到显著偏振，偏振度从5%到超过30%；Cas A、Tycho和SN 1006呈现径向磁场，而RX J1713.7-3946和Vela Jr.呈现切向磁场形态。这种双重行为与演化阶段无简单关联。

Conclusion: IXPE的结果是理解年轻超新星遗迹激波中宇宙线加速过程的重要进展，激波速度和周围介质密度共同影响磁场拓扑结构，挑战了基于射电观测的长期二分法观念。未来观测将进一步约束湍流、激波动力学与粒子加速之间的相互作用。

Abstract: The Imaging X-ray Polarimetry Explorer (IXPE) has opened a new observational window on the physics of supernova remnants (SNRs) by providing the first spatially resolved X-ray polarimetry measurements. These data directly probe the geometry and turbulence of magnetic fields in regions of efficient particle acceleration, thereby constraining models of diffusive shock acceleration and magnetic-field amplification. IXPE has so far observed six young SNRs (Cas A, Tycho, SN 1006, RX J1713.7-3946, Vela Jr., and RCW 86) with published results on the first five. The observations reveal significant polarization in all cases, with degrees of polarization ranging from 5% to over 30%, reflecting different turbulence levels and environmental conditions. Three remnants (Cas A, Tycho, and SN 1006) show predominantly radial magnetic fields, while RX J1713.7-3946 and Vela Jr. display tangential morphologies. This dual behavior, not simply correlated with evolutionary stage, challenges the long-standing dichotomy inferred from radio observations and suggests that both shock velocity and circumstellar medium density play key roles in shaping magnetic-field topology. IXPE's results mark a major step toward disentangling the processes governing cosmic-ray acceleration in young SNR shocks, with ongoing and future observations expected to further constrain the interplay between turbulence, shock dynamics, and particle acceleration.

</details>


### [142] [A Whisper from Within: Response of a Pulsar Timing Array to an Internal Gravitational-wave Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20021)
*Houyuan Qi,Xian Chen,Lin Wang,Kuo Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本论文研究了位于球状星团或星系核内的毫秒脉冲星构成的微型脉冲星计时阵列（mini-PTA）对内部纳赫兹引力波源的探测能力，提出考虑近场效应的综合模型，发现当脉冲星距引力波源足够近时，非辐射场主导导致计时残差急剧增大（可达微秒至毫秒级），远超传统远场近似结果，表明mini-PTA可作为探测中等质量及超大质量黑洞双星等不可见引力波源的强大工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫秒脉冲星在球状星团和星系核中大量存在，有望构成微型脉冲星计时阵列（mini-PTA）探测位于阵列内部的纳赫兹引力波源。然而传统平面波近似在阵列尺度与引力波波长可比时失效，需要引入近场效应（波前曲率、非辐射自场、引力波对脉冲星的直接扰动）来准确描述计时残差。

Method: 构建了包含近场效应的综合模型来计算mini-PTA中由内部引力波源引起的计时残差，考虑波前曲率、非辐射自场及引力波对脉冲星的直接扰动。针对球状星团（M15和ω Centauri）和星系核（Sgr A*和M31）中的实际引力波源配置进行了数值计算。

Result: 当毫秒脉冲星位于距引力波源几个波长以内时，球状星团中计时残差可达1微秒，星系中心可达毫秒量级，处于当前射电望远镜探测能力范围内。当脉冲星位于几个引力波波长以内时，非辐射场占主导，残差随距离的衰减介于1/r_e²到1/r_e⁴之间，远陡于传统远场的1/r_e标度律。

Conclusion: 微型脉冲星计时阵列（mini-PTA）在球状星团或星系核中可作为探测中等质量及超大质量黑洞双星等不可见引力波源的强大探针，近场效应显著增强了探测能力。

Abstract: Millisecond pulsars (MSPs) are abundant in globular clusters (GCs) and probably also in galactic nuclei. They offer the potential to form a miniature pulsar timing array (mini-PTA) to detect nanohertz gravitational-wave (GW) sources located inside the array. Since the size of such an array is comparable to the wavelength of GW, the conventional plane-wave approximation becomes invalid, and near-field effects, including wavefront curvature, non-radiative self-field of the GW source, and direct perturbation of pulsar by GW, become significant. In this work, we incorporate these effects in a comprehensive model to calculate the timing residual induced by a GW source inside a mini-PTA. We also consider realistic GW source configurations in GCs (M15 and $ω$ Centauri) and in galactic nuclei (Sgr A* and M31), and find that for MSPs located sufficiently close to the GW source (within a few wavelengths), the residual can reach $1~μ\mathrm{s}$ in GCs and up to milliseconds in galactic centers, within the potential detection reach of current radio telescopes. Crucially, when the pulsar lies within a few GW wavelengths of the source, the non-radiative field dominates and causes the residual to rise much more steeply (between $1/r_e^2$ and $1/r_e^4$, where $r_e$ is the distance to the source) than the conventional far-field scaling ($1/r_e$). These results demonstrate that mini-PTAs in GCs or galactic nuclei can serve as powerful probes of otherwise invisible GW sources, including intermediate-mass and supermassive black hole binaries.

</details>


### [143] [A FAST search for radio pulsations during the dormant state of the AMSPs IGR J00291+5934 and MAXI J1957+032](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20086)
*A. Marino,E. Parent,F. Coti Zelati,M. C. Baglio,A. Papitto,A. Sanna,A. Anitra,C. Kazantsev,N. Rea,A. Borghese,L. Burderi,T. Di Salvo,C. Espinoza,X. Hou,R. Iaria,G. Illiano,D. M. Russell,R. Sathyaprakash*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用FAST望远镜对两颗吸积毫秒脉冲星（IGR J00291+5934和MAXI J1957+032）在宁静态进行了深度射电观测，未探测到相干周期信号，获得了目前对已知持续吸积毫秒脉冲星最严格的上限（3.3 μJy和5.6 μJy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管过渡毫秒脉冲星在宁静态能观测到射电脉冲，但大多数吸积毫秒脉冲星在宁静态从未被探测到射电脉冲（除IGR J18245-2452外）。本研究旨在对两颗AMSPs进行更灵敏的射电搜索，以检验此类系统在吸积停止后是否也能产生射电脉冲辐射。

Method: 使用FAST望远镜L波段（1-1.5 GHz）对IGR J00291+5934和MAXI J1957+032进行观测；通过Swift和Las Cumbres天文台确认X射线和光学通量的上限以确保源处于宁静态；进行深度相干周期搜索，探测已知自旋频率及其他频率的候选信号。

Result: 未发现任何显著的候选信号；在10%脉冲占空比假设下，分别得到3.3 μJy（IGR J00291+5934）和5.6 μJy（MAXI J1957+032）的脉冲射电通量密度上限——这是目前对已知持续AMSP最严格的限制。

Conclusion: 即使在FAST的高灵敏度观测下，这两颗AMSPs在宁静态仍未被探测到射电脉冲，进一步支持了AMSPs与tMSPs在宁静态射电行为存在本质差异的观点，或表明其射电辐射机制（如束流几何、脉冲宽度等）不利于探测。

Abstract: Accreting millisecond pulsars (AMSPs) and transitional millisecond pulsars (tMSPs) are neutron star low-mass X-ray binaries which can evolve into "recycled" radio millisecond pulsars. In both types of systems, X-ray pulsations have been detected during phases of X-ray activity when matter accretion through a disc is turned on. On the other hand, when accretion stops, and these systems enter the quiescent, low-luminosity X-ray state, only tMSPs become visible as radio pulsars. Despite several attempts, radio pulsations have never been detected in quiescent AMSPs, except for IGR J18245$-$2452. In this manuscript, we present the results of two observational campaigns performed on the AMSPs IGR J00291+5934 and MAXI J1957+032 with the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical Telescope ($\it{FAST}$) in L-band (1-1.5 GHz). Both sources have most likely been observed in quiescence, as suggested by the upper limits on their X-ray and optical flux obtained with Swift and the Las Cumbres Observatory, respectively. We have performed a deep search for coherent periodicities in radio but found no significant candidate signal, either at the known spin frequency of those sources or at other frequencies. Assuming a pulse duty cycle of 10%, we derive upper limits on the pulsed radio flux density of 3.3 $μ$Jy and 5.6 $μ$Jy for IGR J00291+5934 and MAXI J1957+032, respectively, which are the most stringent limits so far for any known persistent AMSP.

</details>


### [144] [On the impact of the carbon fusion rate over the properties of superbursts -- Numerical simulations of superbursts with MESA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20088)
*Martin Nava-Callejas,Stéphane Goriely,Nicolas Chamel*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了碳聚变反应速率的变化对中子星超级爆发的物理影响，发现速率提高或降低1000倍会显著改变爆发周期、衰减时间、点火深度、最高温度及α核素合成，其效果与改变包层底部基底加热相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来对碳聚变反应速率的重新评估发现，其在超爆发温度（T≤10^9 K）下可能与经典速率相差高达1000倍，因此需要探明这种不确定性对超爆发物理过程的具体影响。

Method: 使用公开代码MESA v.24.08.1，采用四种不同版本的碳聚变反应速率，对低质量X射线双星中的中子星超爆发进行数值模拟。

Result: 碳聚变速率提高1000倍会缩短超爆发的复发时间、衰减时间及点火柱深；降低1000倍则产生相反效果。爆发最高温度随之变化，进而影响α核素的合成量级。这些变化与减少包层底部基底加热的效果相当。

Conclusion: 碳聚变反应速率的不确定性对超爆发各个关键物理量均有显著影响，必须在理论模型中予以充分考虑。

Abstract: Context: Superbursts are very energetic explosions in the crust of neutron stars in Low-Mass X-ray Binaries (LMXBs). These are triggered by unstable carbon burning at $T\leq 10^{9}$ K. In recent years, there has been a re-examination of the carbon fusion rate, finding that at these temperatures it might be either smaller or higher with respect to the classic rate from Caughler \& Fowler (1988) by a factor $10^{3}$. Aims: We explore the consequences changing the carbon fusion rate has over the physics of superbursts. Methods: For simulating superbursts, we employ the public code MESA v.24.08.1, as well as four versions of the carbon fusion reaction rate. Results: An enhancement of the reaction rate by a factor $10^{3}$ at $T\leq 10^9$~K reduces the recurrence and decay times of the superburst, as well as the column depth at ignition. The opposite behavior is observed when the carbon fusion rate is reduced by the same factor. The maximum temperature reached during the explosion is also sensitive to these changes, leading to either an enhancement or a reduction in the synthesis of $α$-nuclides. These changes are comparable to the effect of reducing the amount of base heating at the bottom of the envelope.

</details>


### [145] [Kick bimodality of neutron stars and mode dependence of their parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20201)
*Anton D. Lazarev,Sergei B. Popov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对约200颗正常孤立射电脉冲星的分析，发现约30%属于低速度模式，并揭示了低速度模式与低磁场强度（B≲10¹² G）之间的显著关联，高速度模式中未发现磁场低于10¹¹ G的脉冲星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星在诞生时获得的速度（natal velocity kick）分布被观测和理论建模倾向于双峰分布，但不同速度模式下的脉冲星性质差异尚不明确，特别是磁场与速度模式的关联有待探究。

Method: 选取约200颗自转和运动学参数精确确定的正常孤立射电脉冲星，根据双峰速度分布将其划分为低速度模式和高速度模式，比较两组在特征年龄、距离、射电光度、脉冲宽度和磁场强度等参数上的差异，并分析选择效应的影响。

Result: 约30%脉冲星属于低速度模式；特征年龄、距离和射电光度在两模式间存在差异但可归因于选择偏差；脉冲宽度无显著差异；低速度模式中低磁场（B≲10¹² G）脉冲星占比显著更高，磁场低于10¹¹ G的脉冲星均属于低速度模式。

Conclusion: 脉冲星的速度模式与磁场强度存在关联，但该关联的起源尚不清楚，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Analysis of observational data and theoretical modeling favors a bimodal distribution of the natal velocity kick of neutron stars. For $\sim200$ normal isolated radio pulsars with well-determined spin and kinematic parameters, we determine if they belong to the low- or high-velocity mode of the distribution. Our results demonstrate that about $30\%$ belong to the low-velocity mode. We then analyze the differences in the properties of the two sets of pulsars. For some parameters (characteristic ages, distances, and radio luminosities), we see a clear difference between the two modes. However, for these quantities, it can be easily attributed to selection bias. For those parameters that are not a subject of strong selection, such as pulse width, we do not observe any difference. Interestingly, we detect a significant difference in the magnetic field distribution between the two modes. Lower field pulsars ($B\lesssim 10^{12}$~G) are overabundant among objects from the low-velocity mode in comparison to the high-velocity one. Among pulsars with low field ($\lesssim 10^{11}$~G), we do not identify any objects from the high-velocity mode of the kick distribution. The origin of this discrepancy is not clear, and we discuss several possibilities.

</details>


### [146] [Evidence for candidate X-ray pulsations from the ultraluminous X-ray source NGC 7456 ULX-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20308)
*Yuanle Yao,Xiang-Dong Li,Xiao-Jie Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在NGC7456 ULX-1（一个超亮X射线源）中探测到约0.22 Hz的候选脉动信号，表明其中可能存在吸积中子星，表面偶极磁场约为10¹²-10¹⁴ G。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定NGC7456 ULX-1的致密天体性质，验证其是否包含中子星而非黑洞，以理解超亮X射线源的物理本质。

Method: 利用2023年XMM-Newton观测数据，采用加速搜索、Z²_n统计量和轨道解调分析等多种独立时序技术，在考虑双星运动影响下恢复相位相干性以识别脉动信号。

Result: 探测到约0.22 Hz的候选脉动信号，观测期间频率发生漂移，表明吸积转矩驱动的快速自旋演化；估计表面偶极磁场强度为10¹²-10¹⁴ G。

Conclusion: NGC7456 ULX-1可能包含一颗吸积中子星，但需独立数据集或额外观测加以确认。

Abstract: We report evidence for a candidate pulsational signal at $\sim0.22$~Hz from NGC7456 ULX-1, a previously identified ultraluminous X-ray source (ULX). The signal is identified in the 2023 XMM-Newton observation using independent timing techniques including accelerated searches, $Z^2_n$ statistics, and an orbital-demodulation analysis designed to restore phase coherence in the presence of binary motion. The candidate pulsation frequency drift within the observation suggests rapid spin evolution driven by accretion torque. We further estimate the surface dipole magnetic field strength to be $B\sim 10^{12}-10^{14}$ G. These results provide evidence that NGC7456 ULX-1 may host an accreting neutron star, although confirmation with independent datasets or additional observations is required.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [The Kernel of the $\hat A$-Genus in Rational Spin Bordism is Generated by Ricci-Positive Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19505)
*Gerald Höhn,Philipp Höhn*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了有理Spin配边类中，能由具有正Ricci曲率度量的流形表示的类恰好是$\hat A$-属的核。证明采用奇数次二次曲面的光滑完全交$Y_{m,\ell}$，通过$\hat A$-属的一阶加厚诱导线性泛函，并利用多项式插值论证证明满秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答在复椭圆亏格背景下提出的关于正Ricci曲率有理配边障碍的问题，即在可微分有理Spin范畴中刻画能承载正Ricci曲率度量的Spin流形的配边类。

Method: 构造奇数次（$\ell=1,3,\ldots,2m-1$）二次曲面的光滑完全交$Y_{m,\ell}\subset\mathbb{CP}^{2m+\ell}$，这些流形是Spin且Fano的，故具有正Ricci曲率度量。对$\hat A$-属进行一阶加厚，在$(J/J^2)_{4m}$上诱导$m-1$个线性泛函，其在$[Y_{m,\ell}]$上的取值由次数严格递增的多项式$P_{m,q}(\ell)$控制，通过多项式插值证明满秩。

Result: 在每个度数上，有理Spin配边类中正Ricci曲率流形的$\mathbb{Q}$-张成空间恰好等于$\hat A$-属的核$J$。

Conclusion: 完整回答了正Ricci曲率的有理配边障碍问题，表明$\hat A$-属是正Ricci曲率在有理Spin配边群中的唯一障碍。

Abstract: We prove that, in every degree, the rational Spin bordism classes represented by manifolds admitting metrics with positive Ricci curvature span exactly the kernel of the $\hat A$-genus. More precisely, for \[ R=Ω_*^{Spin}\otimes\mathbb{Q},\qquad J=\ker(\hat A:R\longrightarrow\mathbb{Q}[u]),\] the $\mathbb{Q}$-span of bordism classes of Ricci-positive Spin manifolds equals $J$ in each degree. This answers, in the differentiable rational Spin category, a question about rational bordism obstructions to positive Ricci curvature which was raised in the context of complex elliptic genera.
  The proof uses smooth complete intersections of an odd number $\ell$ of quadrics \[ Y_{m,\ell}\subset \mathbb{CP}^{2m+\ell}, \qquad \ell=1,\, 3,\, \ldots,\, 2m-1. \] These manifolds have real dimension $4m$, are Spin and Fano, and therefore admit metrics with positive Ricci curvature. A first-order thickening of the $\hat A$-genus induces $m-1$ linear functionals on $(J/J^2)_{4m}$. Their values on the classes $[Y_{m,\ell}]$ are governed by polynomials $P_{m,q}(\ell)$ of strictly increasing degrees $q+1=1$, $2$, $\ldots$, $m-1$. This gives full rank by a polynomial-interpolation argument.

</details>


### [148] [$K$-Theoretic Obstructions to Linearizing QCA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19657)
*Mattie Ji,Bowen Yang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究量子细胞自动机（QCA）表示的线性化问题，利用代数K-理论谱构建障碍理论，并在复数域上完全计算了点、线和平面上的QCA空间同伦类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射影表示能否线性化是表示论和物理中的基本问题，本文旨在将这一经典问题推广到具有局域性约束的量子细胞自动机表示中，研究其线性化的可行性。

Method: 利用作者先前工作中构建的QCA代数K-理论谱，发展任意域上的障碍理论，通过QCA空间的同伦类型提取线性化的普适障碍类。

Result: 建立了QCA表示线性化的完整障碍理论，并在复数代数与酉情形下，完全计算了点、线、平面上QCA空间的同伦类型。

Conclusion: QCA表示的线性化问题可由代数K-理论谱导出的障碍类完整刻画，且特定情况下的同伦类型可完全确定。

Abstract: Projective representations arise naturally in physics and representation theory, and determining whether they can be linearized has been a fundamental problem. In this work, we study the analogous problem for quantum cellular automata (QCA) representations, which incorporate locality constraints imposed by a metric space $X$. Over an arbitrary field $\mathbb{F}$, we develop an obstruction theory for the linearization of QCA representations, using the algebraic $K$-theory spectrum of QCA constructed in previous work of the authors. The resulting obstructions are governed by the homotopy type of the QCA spaces, from which we extract universal obstruction classes to linearization. In the complex algebraic and unitary case, we also fully compute the homotopy types of the QCA spaces over a point, a line, and a plane.

</details>


### [149] [Configuration spaces and the Arone--Mahowald theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20248)
*Ben Knudsen,Dezhou Li*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Fred Cohen发起的欧几里得配置空间的Cartan-Leray谱序列，建立了原子谱序列直和分解，并由此直接恢复了Arone-Mahowald关于恒等函子Goodwillie导子消失的困难定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续Fred Cohen关于欧几里得配置空间的Cartan-Leray谱序列的研究，并简化Arone-Mahowald重要定理的证明。

Method: 建立欧几里得配置空间Cartan-Leray谱序列的原子谱序列直和分解。

Result: 获得了原子谱序列的直和分解结构，并作为直接推论得到Arone-Mahowald关于恒等函子Goodwillie导子消失的定理。

Conclusion: 该分解为理解配置空间的同调性质提供了新工具，并简化了已知重要结果的证明。

Abstract: We take up the study, initiated by Fred Cohen, of the Cartan--Leray spectral sequence for Euclidean configuration spaces, establishing a decomposition as a direct sum of atomic spectral sequences. As an immediate consequence, we recover a difficult theorem of Arone--Mahowald on the vanishing of Goodwillie derivatives of the identity.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [150] [Universal Properties of Nonlinearly Perturbed Maxwell Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19845)
*Tengyang Liu,Yisong Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类广义非线性微扰麦克斯韦电磁理论具有三个普适基本性质：（i）有限能量的点电荷；（ii）排除有限能量的磁单极子和双荷子；（iii）点电荷有效半径可任意小及其局部不可探测性，为电子作为点电荷的不可见性提供了经典解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一类尽可能广义的非线性微扰麦克斯韦理论，使得该理论能够同时保持三个普适的基本性质，并试图从经典角度解释电子作为点电荷的不可见性。

Method: 对麦克斯韦理论进行广义非线性微扰，选取尽可能一般的非线性轮廓，使得三个普适性质（有限能量点电荷、排除磁单极子/双荷子、有效半径任意小）得以保持。

Result: 该非线性理论能够：（i）支持有限能量的点电荷；（ii）排除有限能量的磁单极子和双荷子；（iii）点电荷有效半径可任意小，且电荷和能量在局部不可探测，从而从经典层面解释了电子作为点电荷的不可见性。

Conclusion: 该理论通过最小化地微扰麦克斯韦理论，以最一般的非线性形式同时实现了三个普适性质，为电子的点电荷特性及其有效半径的极小性提供了经典理论解释。

Abstract: We show that a general nonlinearly perturbed Maxwell theory of electromagnetism possesses three universal fundamental properties:
  (i) A finite-energy electric point charge.
  (ii) Exclusion of finite-energy magnetic monopoles and dually charged dyons.
  (iii) Arbitrary smallness of the effective radius of a point electric charge and the associated local undetectedness of the electric charge and energy.
  In particular, this last property offers a classical explanation for the invisibility of the electron, as a point electric charge, in accordance with the smallness of its effective radius.
  This nonlinear theory of electromagnetism has the feature that it minimally perturbs the Maxwell theory with a nonlinearity profile that is as general as possible such that the three universal properties stated above are all maintained.

</details>


### [151] [Dirac structures on tangent bundles: a geometric framework for variational principles, constrained dynamics, and symmetry reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20012)
*Hiroaki Yoshimura*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种由拉格朗日二形式与约束分布诱导的切丛上的Dirac结构（称为拉格朗日–Dirac结构），为包含非完整、退化拉格朗日和对称系统在内的拉格朗日–Dirac动力学系统提供了统一几何框架，并建立了相应的变分原理、约化理论及无穷维推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为处理非完整约束、退化拉格朗日量以及具有对称性的约束力学系统提供一个统一的几何框架，克服传统方法在退化情形下的局限，并揭示非完整动力学中Dirac结构的规范协变性。

Method: 在切丛上利用拉格朗日二形式与约束分布构造拉格朗日–Dirac结构；通过规范变换分析非完整动力学对二形式的非保持性；在切丛上直接建立拉格朗日–d'Alembert–Dirac变分原理；利用李群对称性发展约化理论，得到约化Dirac结构及相应的欧拉–庞加莱–Dirac方程。

Result: 在超正则情形下，系统恢复为拉格朗日–d'Alembert方程的一阶形式；非完整动力学虽然不保持拉格朗日二形式，但底层Dirac结构在规范变换下保持；约化理论导出了李代数上的约化Dirac结构与欧拉–庞加莱–Dirac方程；变分原理在无约束和超正则约束情形分别退化为Hamilton原理和拉格朗日–d'Alembert原理；以带电粒子、电路、速度线性拉格朗日系统及理想流体（欧拉方程）为例验证了理论。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日–Dirac结构为约束力学系统提供了统一且内在的几何描述，揭示了非完整动力学的规范协变性，并自然推广到约化系统和无穷维连续介质力学系统。

Abstract: We introduce a Dirac structure on the tangent bundle of a configuration manifold, called a \textit{Lagrange--Dirac structure}, which is naturally induced by the Lagrangian two-form associated with a (possibly degenerate) Lagrangian and a constraint distribution. This structure provides a unified geometric framework for Lagrange--Dirac dynamical systems, encompassing nonholonomic, degenerate Lagrangian, and symmetric systems. In the hyperregular case, the system recovers a first-order formulation of the Lagrange--d'Alembert equations. Although nonholonomic dynamics does not preserve the Lagrangian two-form, we show that the underlying Lagrange--Dirac structure is preserved up to gauge transformations, yielding a natural gauge covariance property.
  We also formulate an intrinsic variational principle directly on the tangent bundle, referred to as the \textit{Lagrange--d'Alembert--Dirac principle}, which recovers Hamilton's principle in the unconstrained case and the Lagrange--d'Alembert principle in the hyperregular constrained case. Furthermore, we develop a reduction theory for systems with Lie group symmetry, deriving a reduced Lagrange--Dirac structure over the Lie algebra that yields the Euler--Poincaré--Dirac equations and a corresponding reduced variational principle. Finally, we illustrate the theory through examples including charged particles, electric circuits, and systems with Lagrangians linear in velocity, and present an infinite-dimensional extension to ideal fluids that naturally incorporates the incompressibility constraint and recovers the Euler equations.

</details>


### [152] [On the Emergence of Discrete Spectrum for Weakly Disordered Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20505)
*Stanislav Molchanov,Oleg Safronov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究安德森算子受局部负势扰动后的谱性质，证明吸引势诱导的负特征值（束缚态）数量的精确估计，揭示随机性如何增强特征值边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究安德森局域化与吸引势束缚能力之间的竞争机制，量化随机势对离散谱的影响。

Method: 分析随机薛定谔算子的谱，建立负特征值数量的精确估计，利用局域化与势阱的相互作用给出定量边界。

Result: 得到了受局部负势扰动的安德森算子的负特征值数量的精确界限。

Conclusion: 随机性能够增强特征值边界，为理解无序系统中的束缚态提供新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral properties of the Anderson operator perturbed by a localized negative potential, \(-V\). Specifically, we analyze the random Schrödinger operator defined by \(H = -Δ+\ve \sum_{n} ω_n χ_n - V\), where the unperturbed operator exhibits a disordered energy landscape. Our primary focus is to establish precise estimates on the number of negative eigenvalues (bound states) induced by the attractive perturbation. By analyzing the competition between Anderson localization and the binding capacity of the potential, we provide quantitative bounds on the discrete spectrum. These results offer new insights into how randomness enhances the eigenvalue bounds.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Semi-Automatic Correction of 3D Tubular Structure Skeletons via Component-Wise MST and Filtered Delaunay Triangulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19949)
*Ruoxuan Yang,Chuan Li*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种半自动修正方法，通过用户选择源点和目标点，结合最小生成树和3D Delaunay边图，从3D管状结构骨架中重建合理的中心线连接，用于修正拓扑伪影（如错误连接和断裂）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D管状结构骨架化常引入拓扑伪影（如分支间错误连接、中心线断裂），手动修正耗时且易出错，需要一种从少量用户输入即可自动重建合理连接的方法。

Method: 用户选择源点和目标点后，方法通过结合（i）分量级最小生成树实现稳定局部传播，和（ii）滤波后的3D Delaunay边图用于桥接间隙和处理模糊连接点。候选步骤通过考虑方向连续性、空间邻近性、分量一致性和目标导向进展的评分进行排序，输出有序多段线作为建议修正。

Result: 在脑血管数据集上展示了定性结果，成功修正了典型的"交叉"和"点状"伪影。当前验证为定性性质。

Conclusion: 该方法轻量级，可作为生物医学成像及相关领域更全面的交互式修正管道的实用构建模块。

Abstract: Skeletonization of tubular structures from 3D imaging is essential for tasks such as morphometric analysis, transport or flow simulation, and procedural planning in domains including vascular networks, plant root systems, and neural connectomes. However, automatic skeleton extraction often introduces topological artifacts, such as erroneous connections between nearby branches and fragmented centerlines caused by noise or missing data. Correcting these artifacts manually can be time-consuming and error-prone, especially when precise interaction is required.
  We present a semi-automatic correction method that reconstructs a plausible centerline connection from minimal user input. Given a user-selected source and target point, our method traces a path by combining (i) component-wise minimum spanning trees for stable local propagation and (ii) a filtered 3D Delaunay edge graph for bridging gaps and handling ambiguous junctions. Candidate steps are ranked using a score that accounts for direction continuity, spatial proximity, component consistency, and target-directed progress. The output is an ordered polyline (or edge sequence) that can be used as a suggested correction and integrated into downstream skeleton post-processing workflows.
  We implement the system in C++ with an interactive viewer based on Libigl and demonstrate representative qualitative results on brain vessel datasets, including correction of typical "crossing" and "dotted" artifacts. While our current validation is qualitative, the method is lightweight and serves as a practical building block toward more comprehensive interactive correction pipelines in biomedical imaging and related domains.

</details>


### [154] [Quadratic Forms for Measuring Geometric Trees in 3-dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20096)
*Yossi Bokor Bleile,Emanuele Cortinovis,Herbert Edelsbrunner,Shota Uka*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用二次型理论测量几何图的方向分布，并引入基于Fisher度量的六边形图模型来可视化、测量和统计树状结构的方向特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状结构在科学中广泛存在，其形状有助于理解潜在的驱动或产生过程，但缺乏有效的几何和拓扑工具来系统研究这些结构的方向分布特性。

Method: 将树状结构视为R^3中的几何图，采用二次型理论测量其方向分布，并引入六边形图模型，该模型配备基于标准三角形上Fisher度量的度量。

Result: 提出了一种新的可视化、测量和统计几何图方向分布的方法框架。

Conclusion: 通过结合计算几何、拓扑学以及信息几何的工具，为树状结构的方向分析提供了一种新颖的定量和可视化方法。

Abstract: Tree-like structures appear in many areas of science, and their shapes can help understand the underlying processes they drive or that give rise to them.
  By thinking of these structures as geometric graphs in $\mathbb{R}^3$, we gain access to tools from computational geometry and topology to study them.
  In this paper, we adopt the theory of quadratic forms to measure the directional spread of geometric graphs, and we introduce the hexplot model -- equipped with a metric derived from the Fisher metric on the standard triangle -- to visualize, measure, and collect statistics.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Covariate-Adjusted Functional Principal Components Analysis for Modeling Hazard Rates of Physical Activity in the US Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19760)
*Md Rokibul Hasan,Pratim Guha Niyogi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于风险函数的分布分析框架，通过腕戴加速度计数据建模个体活动强度模式，利用功能主成分分析（FPCA）刻画活动强度分布的变异，发现该方法比均值汇总更能有效捕捉个体间差异，尤其在高强度活动层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均值类汇总指标无法描述体力活动在整个分布上的模式，而个体间的活动强度分布存在显著异质性，亟需一种能够刻画完整分布特征的解析方法。

Method: 基于生存分析思路，为每个个体在公共强度网格上推导非参数活动强度风险函数；将原始MIMS及其对数变换后的风险曲线视为函数型数据对象，分别采用功能主成分分析（FPCA）提取主要变异模式。

Result: 组平均风险函数在低强度水平差异较小，而在高强度水平差异显著；基于风险函数的函数表示比均值汇总更能有效捕捉个体间差异。

Conclusion: 基于风险函数的函数型表示是一种灵活且可解释的方法，能够更好地刻画体力活动强度分布的异质性，支持不同人群亚组间的原则性比较。

Abstract: Physical activity plays a vital role in human health. Its entire distribution differs among people. Commonly used summary measures cannot describe this distributional pattern. We present a distribution-based analytical approach to describe physical activity by modeling individual-level activity-intensity patterns through hazard functions derived from wrist-worn accelerometer data. We analyzed minute-level Monitor-Independent Movement Summary (MIMS) data of 4297 adults with seven continuous days of device wear from the 2011- 2012 National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES). We derived a nonparametric activity-intensity hazard using a survival-based approach for each individual on a common intensity grid, treating both the hazard curves from MIMS and their log-transformed MIMS as functional objects. We used functional principal component analysis (FPCA) on both scales of MIMS to characterize dominant modes of variation in activity-intensity distributions. Group-wise mean hazard functions showed little difference at lower intensity levels, while we observed a substantial difference at higher intensity levels. Our results demonstrate that hazard-based functional representations for capturing differences in physical activity intensity distributions across individuals offer a flexible and interpretable way to characterize heterogeneity. This approach works better than mean-based summaries and supports principled comparisons of physical activity patterns across population subgroups.
